Recently, decision trees became customary to represent controllers and policies. However, since shields are inherently non-deterministic, their decision tree representations become too large to be explainable in practice. To address this challenge, we propose a novel approach for explainable safe RL that enhances trust by providing human-interpretable explanations of the shield's decisions. Our method represents the shielding policy as a hierarchy of decision trees, offering top-down, case-based explanations. At design time, we use a world model to analyze the safety risks of executing actions in given states. Based on this analysis, we construct both the shield and a high-level decision tree that classifies states into risk categories (safe, critical, dangerous, unsafe), explaining why a situation may be safety-critical. At runtime, we generate localized decision trees that explain which actions are allowed and why others are deemed unsafe. Our method facilitates explainability of the safety aspect in safe-by-shielding reinforcement learning, requires no additional information beyond what is already used for shielding, incurs minimal overhead, and integrates readily into existing shielded RL pipelines. In our experiments, we compute explanations using decision trees that are several orders of magnitude smaller than the original shield.

</details>


### [142] [ALINC: Active Learning for Inductive Node Classification via Graph Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04647)
*Pascal Plettenberg,Denis Huseljic,André Alcalde,Bernhard Sick,Josephine M. Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ALINC框架，解决了归纳式节点分类中需要以整个图为单位进行主动学习选择的问题，通过聚合机制将节点级效用指标提升为图级选择标准，实验证明CoreSet、TypiClust和BADGE是最优策略，且聚合方法的选择对性能和标注成本有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习主要针对单图或少数大图中的节点选择，但在分子化学、电子设计自动化等领域，数据集由数千个独立图组成，标注单个节点需要全图分析，从而自动获得其余节点标签。因此需要选择整个图而非单个节点的主动学习策略，而此前文献中尚未解决该问题。

Method: ALINC框架通过多种聚合机制（三种聚合方法）将节点级效用度量提升为图级选择标准，实现图级别的采样。在四个数据集上对十种策略和三种聚合方法进行了全面基准测试。

Result: CoreSet、TypiClust和BADGE是表现最佳的图采样策略；聚合方法的选择对模型性能和标注成本有显著影响；在分子位点代谢预测和印刷电路板原理图设计自动化两个用例中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: ALINC成功填补了归纳式节点分类中图级主动学习的方法论空白，为处理独立图数据集的主动学习提供了有效框架，且聚合机制是关键设计因素。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) for node classification typically focuses on selecting the most informative nodes for annotation within one or a few large graphs (e.g., in social network analysis). However, in other domains, such as molecular chemistry or electronic design automation, datasets consist of thousands of independent graphs. In many of these inductive settings, annotating an individual node requires a full-graph analysis, which effectively yields the remaining node labels on-the-fly. Therefore, these scenarios require AL strategies that select entire graphs instead of single nodes, a problem which has not been tackled in the literature so far. Thus, we introduce ALINC, an AL framework for inductive node classification via graph sampling. It bridges the existing methodological gap by elevating node-level utility measures to graph-level selection criteria through various aggregation mechanisms. In an extensive benchmark including ten strategies, three aggregation methods, and four datasets, we identify CoreSet, TypiClust, and BADGE as the top-performing graph sampling strategies. Our detailed analysis further reveals that the choice of the aggregation method is pivotal, as it substantially affects model performance and annotation costs. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of ALINC in two use case studies: site-of-metabolism prediction in molecules and design automation of printed circuit board schematics.

</details>


### [143] [Why Muon Outperforms Adam: A Curvature Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04662)
*Shuche Wang,Fengzhuo Zhang,Jiaxiang Li,Dirk Bergemann,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从曲率视角首次解释Muon优化器相比Adam在LLM训练中效率翻倍的原因：Muon通过更小的归一化方向锐度（NDS）实现更低的二阶曲率惩罚，从而获得更大的单步损失下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大语言模型训练中效率约为Adam的两倍，但其局部几何优势的来源尚不清楚，本文旨在从曲率角度揭开这一谜团。

Method: 利用二阶泰勒近似分析训练损失景观，将曲率惩罚分解为更新范数平方与归一化方向锐度（NDS）的乘积；使用Zipf-PCFG数据控制数据不平衡程度进行实验；对层内/层间曲率分解；在具有异质曲率和梯度对齐的二次问题上进行理论分析。

Result: Muon与Adam一阶收益相当，但Muon的二阶曲率惩罚更小；曲率惩罚差异主要由更低的NDS驱动而非更新尺度；数据不平衡放大Muon的NDS优势；中后期训练中Muon的低NDS主要由更小的层内曲率维持；理论上证明Muon通过平衡不同曲率组间的更新能量获得更小的平均NDS。

Conclusion: Muon优于Adam的核心在于其更新方向在曲率方向上的分布更均衡（NDS更低），从而在相同更新步长下更少受制于损失景观的高曲率方向，实现更高效的单步下降。

Abstract: Muon improves training efficiency over Adam in large language-model training by about two times, but the local geometric source of this advantage remains unclear. Our work takes a first step toward demystifying Muon's superiority over Adam from a curvature perspective. First, we apply a second-order Taylor approximation to the training landscape and show that Muon achieves a larger one-step loss decrease than Adam at matched validation loss. The two optimizers have comparable first-order gains, but Muon consistently incurs a smaller second-order curvature penalty. Second, we decompose this curvature penalty into the squared update norm and Normalized Directional Sharpness (NDS). We find that Muon and Adam have comparable update norms, so Muon's smaller curvature penalty is driven by lower NDS, not update scale. Third, we study how training data and model structure shape Muon's NDS advantage. Using Zipf-Probabilistic Context-Free Grammar (PCFG) data with controlled imbalance, we show that data imbalance amplifies Muon's NDS advantage over Adam. A within-/cross-layer decomposition further shows that, in the middle and late stages of training, Muon's lower NDS is mainly sustained by smaller within-layer curvature. Beyond empirical evidence, we analyze stylized quadratic problems with heterogeneous curvature and gradient alignment toward high-curvature modes. We prove that Muon attains a smaller average NDS than GD by balancing update energy across curvature groups; when curvature heterogeneity is sufficiently strong, this also yields lower local quadratic loss after the same number of steps.

</details>


### [144] [Towards Accurate Model Selection in Deep Unsupervised Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04665)
*Kaichao You,Ximei Wang,Mingsheng Long,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度无监督域适应（Deep UDA）中缺乏准确、标准化的模型选择方法的问题，本文提出Deep Embedded Validation (DEV)，通过将自适应特征表示嵌入验证过程，实现对目标风险的无偏估计且方差有界，并利用控制变量技术进一步降低方差，理论证明与实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度无监督域适应领域缺乏准确且标准化的模型选择方法，现有方法存在高偏差、受限、不稳定甚至争议性（需要标注目标数据）等问题，阻碍了该领域的进一步发展。

Method: 提出Deep Embedded Validation (DEV)，将自适应特征表示嵌入验证过程，实现对目标风险的无偏估计且方差有界；进一步采用控制变量技术降低方差。

Result: 该方法实现了对目标风险的无偏估计，方差有界，并通过控制变量技术进一步降低了方差。

Conclusion: DEV方法在理论和实验上均被证明是有效的，解决了Deep UDA中模型选择的难题。

Abstract: Deep unsupervised domain adaptation (Deep UDA) methods successfully leverage rich labeled data in a source domain to boost the performance on related but unlabeled data in a target domain. However, algorithm comparison is cumbersome in Deep UDA due to the absence of accurate and standardized model selection method, posing an obstacle to further advances in the field. Existing model selection methods for Deep UDA are either highly biased, restricted, unstable, or even controversial (requiring labeled target data). To this end, we propose \textit{Deep Embedded Validation} (\textbf{DEV}), which embeds adapted feature representation into the validation procedure to obtain unbiased estimation of the target risk with bounded variance. The variance is further reduced by the technique of control variate. The efficacy of the method has been justified both theoretically and empirically.

</details>


### [145] [Learning Long Range Spatio-Temporal Representations over Continuous Time Dynamic Graphs with State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04672)
*Ayushman Raghuvanshi,Thummaluru Siddartha Readdy,Sundeep Prabhakar Chepuri,Mahesh Chandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于状态空间模型的连续时间动态图框架（CTDG-SSM），通过引入拓扑感知的高阶多项式投影算子（CTT-HiPPO）实现长程时空信息传播，在动态链接预测、节点分类和序列分类任务上达到SOTA，尤其在需要长程时空推理的数据集上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTDG方法受限于捕获一跳或局部时间邻域信息，无法有效捕捉多跳或全局结构模式，特别是在长时域信息保留与更新方面存在不足。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，提出CTT-HiPPO记忆算子，将经典HiPPO解通过拉普拉斯矩阵多项式进行投影，实现拓扑感知的记忆更新；进而推导出连续时间动态图的等效状态空间模型（CTDG-SSM），并采用零阶保持方法得到计算高效的离散形式。

Result: 在动态链接预测、动态节点分类和序列分类基准上取得SOTA性能，尤其在需要长程时间与空间推理的数据集上获得大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: CTDG-SSM通过将图拓扑信息融入状态空间建模，有效解决了CTDG中的长程信息传播问题，为动态图表示学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDGs) provide a richer framework to capture fine-grained temporal patterns in evolving relational data. Long-range information propagation is a key challenge while learning representations, wherein it is important to retain and update information over long temporal horizons. Existing approaches restrict models to capture one-hop or local temporal neighborhoods and fail to capture multi-hop or global structural patterns. To mitigate this, we derive a parameter-efficient state-space modeling framework for continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDG-SSM) from first principles. We first introduce continuous-time Topology-Aware higher order polynomial projection operator (CTT-HiPPO), a novel memory-based reformulation of HiPPO to jointly encode temporal dynamics and graph structure. The solution from CTT-HiPPO is obtained by projecting the classical HiPPO solution through a polynomial of the Laplacian matrix, yielding topology-aware memory updates that admit an equivalent state-space formulation for CTDGs (CTDG-SSM). Then a computationally efficient discrete formulation is obtained using the zero-order hold approach for model implementation.
  Across benchmarks on dynamic link prediction, dynamic node classification, and sequence classification, CTDG-SSM achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, it achieves large performance gains on datasets that require long range temporal (LRT) and spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [146] [Test-Time Compute Scaling for ASR with Depth-Conditioned Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04678)
*Yacouba Kaloga,Shashi Kumar,Shakeel A. Sheikh,Driss Khalil,Petr Motlicek,Ina Kodrasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LARM是一个深度条件循环Transformer，通过循环复用共享模块，使端到端ASR系统能在测试时通过增加循环次数来提升识别性能，而无需训练更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端ASR系统在推理时使用固定深度的声学编码器，无法在不训练更大模型的情况下通过增加测试时计算量来提升识别性能。

Method: 提出LARM（深度条件循环Transformer），结合稀疏CTC检查点、监督时钟嵌入、FiLM深度条件化和延迟软后验反馈，将循环编码器深度变为可控的测试时计算轴。

Result: 在LibriSpeech上，LARM的WER随推理循环次数增加而持续改善，性能可与更深层、非共享参数的基线模型媲美。

Conclusion: 测试时计算扩展可以超越自回归语言模型推理，扩展到连续非自回归语音识别领域。

Abstract: End-to-end ASR systems typically use fixed-depth acoustic encoders at inference, making it difficult to trade additional test-time computation for improved recognition without training a larger model. A natural approach is to reuse a shared Transformer block recurrently, but we find that naive looping does not fully exploit additional recurrent compute. We introduce LARM, a depth-conditioned looped Transformer that turns recurrent encoder depth into a controllable test-time compute axis. LARM combines sparse CTC checkpoints, supervision-clock embeddings, FiLM depth conditioning, and delayed soft-posterior feedback. These components structure the loop into recognition checkpoints separated by latent refinement phases and allow shared weights to specialize across recurrent steps. On LibriSpeech, LARM improves WER as the number of inference loops increases and achieves performance competitive with deeper unshared-parameter baselines. Our results show that test-time compute scaling can extend beyond autoregressive language-model reasoning to continuous non-autoregressive speech recognition.

</details>


### [147] [Cone-Compatible Monge Geometry for High-Dimensional Ordered Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04695)
*Lei Luo,Hongliang Zhang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于闭凸锥诱导偏序的高维最优传输Monge结构，在满足锥-代价兼容性（尖锥条件）时，对支撑在锥链上的测度可得到类似一维单调重排的闭式最优耦合，并建立了相应的Wasserstein度量理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维最优传输通常缺乏闭式解，而一维情形因实数的序结构与凸传输代价兼容从而可通过单调重排得到显式解。本文试图在更高维度中利用偏序恢复类似的Monge结构。

Method: 引入锥-兼容Monge几何：利用闭凸锥K诱导偏序，定义代价兼容性需满足Monge交换不等式。对平方Mahalanobis代价，证明兼容性等价于K在M-内积下是尖的（锐角锥），即对所有u,v∈K有u^T M v ≥ 0。在此条件下，对支撑在锥链上的测度构造分位数型闭式最优耦合。

Result: 获得了平方Mahalanobis代价下锥-代价兼容性的严格刻画（尖锥条件）；建立了锥链Wasserstein度量及其对偶、稳定性、逼近、高斯恢复和计算性质；区分了锥链Wasserstein度量与扩展有向锥传输代价。

Conclusion: 该理论并非通用的快速替代方法，而是对有序高维数据提供可解释、方向有效、原始空间上的单调传输工具，与切片Wasserstein和树Wasserstein距离互补。

Abstract: High-dimensional optimal transport is seldom available in closed form. The one-dimensional case is exceptional because the order of the real line is compatible with convex transport costs, making monotone rearrangement optimal. This paper studies when an analogous Monge structure can be recovered in higher dimensions from a partial order. We introduce a cone-compatible Monge geometry: a closed convex cone (K) induces the order (x\preceq_K y) whenever (y-x\in K), and is compatible with a cost if ordered pairs satisfy a Monge exchange inequality. For squared Mahalanobis costs (c_M(x,y)=(x-y)^\top M(x-y)), we prove a sharp characterization: compatibility holds exactly when (K) is acute under the (M)-inner product, namely (u^\top Mv\ge0) for all (u,v\in K), equivalently (K\subseteq K_M^*). Under this condition, measures supported on cone chains admit a quantile-type closed-form optimal coupling, yielding exact transport under the original ground cost rather than after projection or metric replacement. We distinguish the resulting cone-chain Wasserstein metric on canonically ordered chain distributions from an extended directed cone transport cost on general measures, and develop feasibility, duality, stability, approximation, Gaussian recovery, statistical, and computational results. The theory is complementary to sliced and tree Wasserstein distances: it is not a universal fast surrogate, but a way to obtain interpretable, direction-valid, original-space monotone transport for ordered high-dimensional data.

</details>


### [148] [Graph-Guided Universum Learning in Generalized Eigenvalue Proximal SVMs for Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04699)
*Yogesh Kumar,Vrushank Ahire,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种图引导的Universum学习模型（UG-GEPSVM和IUG-GEPSVM），通过构建MCI样本的图拉普拉斯正则化来捕获几何结构，用于阿尔茨海默病与正常组的结构MRI分类，在ADNI数据集上优于现有方法，最高平均AUC达88.07%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Universum的GEPSVM方法将Universum样本视为独立点，忽略了样本间的几何关系，无法充分利用MCI（轻度认知障碍）作为中间状态的结构信息。

Method: 使用MCI作为Universum数据，基于高斯相似度、最小生成树连通性和多跳传播构建图，导出拉普拉斯矩阵以捕获MCI样本的几何结构，将拉普拉斯正则化替代传统独立Universum惩罚项。UG-GEPSVM集成到广义特征值框架，IUG-GEPSVM扩展到改进的稳定标准特征值框架。

Result: 在ADNI数据集的ICA和PCA特征上，UG-GEPSVM最高平均AUC达88.07%，在五种噪声水平下性能稳定，统计检验显著优于现有GEPSVM和Universum方法。

Conclusion: 图引导的几何正则化能有效利用MCI样本的流形结构，提升AD分类性能与噪声鲁棒性，验证了捕获Universum样本间几何关系的重要性。

Abstract: Early and accurate detection of Alzheimer's disease (AD) is important for timely intervention and disease management. Generalized Eigenvalue Proximal Support Vector Machine (GEPSVM) and its Universum-based variants have shown promising results for AD classification. However, existing methods treat Universum samples as independent points and do not consider the geometric relationships among them. This paper proposes two graph-guided Universum learning models, namely UG-GEPSVM and IUG-GEPSVM, for AD versus cognitively normal (CN) classification using structural MRI data. In the proposed framework, mild cognitive impairment (MCI) subjects are used as Universum data to provide intermediate information between AD and CN classes. A graph is constructed over the Universum samples using Gaussian similarity, Minimum Spanning Tree connectivity, and multi-hop propagation. From this graph, a Laplacian matrix is derived that captures the geometric structure of the MCI samples. This Laplacian-based regularization is incorporated into the learning process in place of the conventional independent Universum penalty term. UG-GEPSVM integrates this regularization into the generalized eigenvalue formulation, while IUG-GEPSVM extends the numerically stable improved GEPSVM framework using a standard eigenvalue formulation. Experiments on ADNI MRI dataset variants using ICA- and PCA-based features at five different noise levels show that both proposed models consistently outperform existing GEPSVM and Universum-based methods. UG-GEPSVM achieves the highest average AUC of 88.07% and maintains stable performance under increasing noise levels. Statistical tests further confirm the significance of the observed improvements.

</details>


### [149] [Contrastive Learning and Correlation Clustering for Sequences of Network Telescope Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04733)
*Jannik Presberger,Alexander Männel,Maynard Koch,Thomas C. Schmidt,Matthias Wählisch,Bjoern Andres*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需预训练和标注的对比学习方法，利用Transformer模型嵌入网络流记录序列，通过相关性聚类有效识别互联网扫描器之间的语义关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解互联网扫描器的活动具有挑战性，通常需要识别扫描源之间的关系，而语义标注数据稀缺。

Method: 提出一种Transformer模型，对最小预处理的网络流记录序列进行嵌入，并使用对比学习训练；基于学习到的相似度构建相关性聚类问题并局部求解。

Result: 学习到的相似度在同一来源的序列间显著高于不同来源的序列，该性质可泛化到未见过的序列和来源；相关性聚类产生的簇与扫描器标签一致。

Conclusion: 对比学习能够有效估计网络流序列之间的语义关系，无需预训练和标注，可用于互联网扫描器的无监督聚类分析。

Abstract: Understanding activities of Internet scanners is challenging; it often requires identifying relationships between sources, a task for which semantic annotations are scarce. This work investigates whether semantically meaningful pairwise relationships between sequences of network flow records can be estimated by contrastive learning, without pretraining and without annotations. To this end, we propose a transformer model that embeds minimally preprocessed sequences of network flow records and train it using contrastive learning. With the similarities obtained from this model, we state a correlation clustering problem and solve it locally. Experimentally, we show: Learned similarities are higher on average for sequences originating from the same source than for sequences originating from different sources, and this property generalizes to unseen sequences of unseen sources. Moreover, correlation clustering yields clusters consistent with scanner labels. The complete source code of the algorithms and for reproducing the experiments is publicly available.

</details>


### [150] [Trace-Mediated Peak Bias: Bridging Temporal Credit Assignment and Cognitive Heuristics in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04735)
*Viktor Veselý,Aleksandar Todorov,Erwan Escudie,Matthia Sabatelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度强化学习中存在“迹介导峰值偏差”（TMPB），即智能体在中等资格迹深度下会非理性地偏好高奖励峰值而非高累计回报的轨迹。该机制解释了峰终定律，并指出自适应优化器可通过二阶矩归一化缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间信用分配与非线性函数逼近之间的交互作用尚未被充分理解，本文旨在揭示这一交互中存在的系统性失效模式。

Method: 通过理论分析和实验验证，识别出迹介导峰值偏差（TMPB），并分析资格迹如何将远端TD误差放大为“梯度冲击”，导致固定步长SGD无法归一化而产生全局高估。

Result: TMPB会导致智能体非理性地选择高奖励峰值的轨迹；自适应优化器（如Adam）通过二阶矩归一化能有效缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 类人的显著性扭曲可能自然产生于分布式系统中信用分配的数学约束，自适应优化是理性价值估计的理论必要条件。

Abstract: Temporal credit assignment is central to both biological and artificial intelligence, yet its interaction with non-linear function approximation is poorly understood. We identify a systematic failure mode in deep reinforcement learning (RL) termed Trace-Mediated Peak Bias (TMPB). At intermediate eligibility trace depths, agents irrationally prefer trajectories with high-magnitude reward ``peaks'' over alternatives with higher cumulative returns. This provides a mechanistic account of the Peak-End Rule: a human memory bias where experiences are judged by their most intense moments rather than integrated utility. We show that TMPB emerges because traces amplify distal Temporal Difference errors into ``gradient shocks'' that fixed-step-size Stochastic Gradient Descent cannot normalize, leading to global overestimation. Conversely, adaptive optimizers mitigate this pathology via second-moment normalization. Our results suggest that human-like saliency distortions may emerge naturally from the mathematical constraints of credit assignment in distributed systems, and that adaptive optimization is a theoretical necessity for rational value estimation.

</details>


### [151] [Curvature-aware dynamic precision approach for physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04736)
*Yingjie Shao,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis,George van Voorn,Taniya Kapoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种曲率感知精度控制方法，在PINN训练中自适应切换FP32和FP64精度，利用L-BFGS优化器的曲率信息判断何时需要更高精度，从而在保持预测精度的同时降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN实现中，FP32计算高效但易出现失败模式，FP64精度高但计算成本高昂，二者之间存在精度与效率的折衷。需要一种自适应精度策略来兼顾两者优势。

Method: 利用L-BFGS优化器中的曲率信息构建精度控制器：当训练动态显示数值敏感或精度受限停滞时升级到FP64，否则保持FP32计算。

Result: 在四个标准PINN失败模式基准和一个辐照驱动ODE示例上，该方法在匹配甚至略微超过全FP64精度的同时，相比全双精度训练缩短了训练时间。

Conclusion: PINN优化中的精度敏感性具有阶段性特征，仅在数值关键阶段选择性应用高精度可以在不牺牲预测精度的情况下降低计算成本。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have become a promising framework for simulating partial differential equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws directly into neural network training. However, recent studies show that PINN optimisation is sensitive to numerical precision. Existing implementations commonly use either single precision (FP32), which is computationally efficient but prone to failure modes, or double precision (FP64), which is robust but substantially expensive. This creates a trade-off between computational efficiency and numerical accuracy. To reduce the computational cost of double-precision training while retaining prediction accuracy, we propose a curvature-aware precision controller that adapts numerical precision during training rather than treating it as a fixed implementation choice. The proposed method reuses curvature information derived from the limited-memory BFGS (L-BFGS) optimiser to construct a precision controller, retaining FP32 when lower precision is sufficient and promoting computation to FP64 when the training dynamics indicate numerical sensitivity or precision-limited stagnation. We evaluate the proposed approach on four canonical PINN failure-mode benchmarks and an irradiance-driven ordinary differential equation example. We further test the proposed approach across different neural network architectures. The method consistently matches or even slightly exceeds full FP64 solution accuracy while reducing training time relative to full double-precision training on all benchmark equations. The obtained results indicate that precision sensitivity in PINN optimisation is phase-dependent, and that selectively applying higher precision only during numerically critical stages can lower computational cost without sacrificing predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [152] [An Empirical Audit of Input Encoders for Multi-Channel Signal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04752)
*Ossi Lehtinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地审计了八种将多通道标量信号编码为Transformer输入向量的方法，发现标准线性投影（nn.Linear(C, d_model)）在合成和真实基准上与其他复杂方法性能几乎等价，共享标量编码和独立通道编码效果较差，建议默认使用线性投影，仅在特定需求时考虑更复杂方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer处理多通道时序信号时，需要将每时刻C个同时存在的标量值嵌入到一个d_model维向量中，但目前缺乏对不同输入编码方案的系统性比较，研究者不清楚默认选择是否最优，也不了解各方案在实际任务中的相对表现。

Method: 在合成基准（使通道身份信息具备区分性）和ETTh1真实数据上，以负对数似然（NLL）为指标，审计了八种输入编码器：共享标量基线、每通道线性投影、正交正则化、非线性MLP茎、分块拼接、通道独立编码、通道即令牌架构、投影位置编码。

Result: 标准线性投影与所有"上层"替代方案性能几乎等价，差异虽统计显著但实际差距微小。共享标量编码因信息论原因完全失效，独立通道编码在两项基准上均表现不佳且过拟合。投影位置编码在通道数小时略优（通过位置-通道正交化机制），非线性MLP茎在通道数大时略优（但更多数据下差距缩小）。

Conclusion: 建议默认使用nn.Linear(C, d_model)作为通道编码器，仅当任务有明确理由时才考虑更复杂的替代方案。提供了可复现的代码和数据。

Abstract: Transformers consuming multi-channel scalar signals must embed $C$ simultaneous values into one $d_{\text{model}}$-dimensional vector per time step. We empirically audit eight input encoders -- spanning a shared-scalar baseline, per-channel linear projections, an orthogonality regulariser, a nonlinear MLP stem, block-partitioned concatenation, channel-independent and channel-as-token architectures, and a projected positional encoding -- on a synthetic benchmark designed to make channel identity informative and on ETTh1 as a real-data check, measured in next-step negative log-likelihood (NLL). The headline is one of practical near-equivalence within a wide "top tier": the standard per-channel linear projection (nn.Linear(C, $d_{\text{model}}$)) matches every alternative in that tier up to small, statistically real but practically modest, differences. Two encoders lose decisively: the shared-scalar baseline, which collapses for information-theoretic reasons we make explicit, and the channel-independent PatchTST-spirit baseline, which underperforms on both benchmarks and overfits universally on the synthetic one. Paired tests resolve two small gaps: projecting the sinusoidal positional encoding through a learned linear layer edges the rest at small $C$, with a direct geometric probe showing the mechanism is positional-channel orthogonalisation; a nonlinear MLP stem edges them at the largest $C$ we test, with the gap shrinking under more training data. The practical recommendation is to use nn.Linear(C, $d_{\text{model}}$) by default and reach for something more elaborate only when the task at hand gives a real reason to do so. Code and data to reproduce every experiment in this paper are available at https://github.com/OssiLehtinen/channel-encoder-audit

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Structural Symmetries: Linear Mode Connectivity via Neuron Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04754)
*Vincent Bürgin,Daniel Herbst,Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了有效函数类的理论框架，研究了参数对称性与神经元可识别性之间的关系，证明神经网络在结构不对称模型中仍存在大量近似等价解，并发现神经元可识别性能够实现无需预对齐的表示合并，同时刻画了这种合并何时存在线性低损路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管参数对称性在深度学习中受到关注，但参数、数据和表示之间的精确相互作用仍未充分探索，本文旨在弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 构建了有效函数类的理论框架，定义神经元在其输入支撑上可实现的功能集及其范数代价，并通过跨独立训练运行的神经元可识别性来形式化有效对称性破缺。

Result: 神经网络即使在结构不对称模型中也能存在大量近似等价解；神经元可识别性使表示合并无需预对齐成为可能；刻画了表示合并何时存在线性低损路径。

Conclusion: 有效函数类在影响损失景观中扮演关键角色，为理解参数对称性与训练动力学提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Many striking phenomena in deep learning, such as linear mode connectivity and the structured behavior of training dynamics, are closely tied to parameter symmetries: transformations that leave the realized function unchanged. Despite growing attention to parameter symmetries, the exact interplay between parameters, data, and representations remains underexplored. To investigate this, we develop a theoretical framework of effective function classes, i.e., the set of functions a neuron can realize on its input support, and the norm cost of realizing them. We then formalize effective symmetry breaking via neuron identifiability across independent training runs. Our analysis shows that neural networks can admit large families of approximately equivalent solutions even in structurally asymmetric models. We further show that neuron identifiability enables representation merging without prior alignment, and characterize when such merging admits a linear low-loss path. These findings highlight the role of effective function classes in affecting the loss landscape.

</details>


### [154] [Measuring Model Robustness via Fisher Information: Spectral Bounds, Theoretical Guarantees, and Practical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04767)
*Chong Zhang,Xiang Li,Jia Wang,Qiufeng Wang,Xiaobo Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Fisher信息矩阵谱范数的攻击无关鲁棒性度量指标，从理论、算法和实验三方面验证其有效性，可作为可解释的诊断工具指导模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络鲁棒性评估方法高度依赖特定攻击方法且缺乏可解释性，亟需一种攻击无关、理论可解释的鲁棒性度量标准。

Method: 基于Fisher信息矩阵(FIM)的谱范数定义鲁棒性度量，证明FIM等于输入Jacobian的方差；推导VGG、ResNet、DenseNet、Transformer等常见架构的闭式谱界；提出幂迭代和Hutchinson估计算法支持白盒和黑盒场景。

Result: 在CIFAR、ImageNet、医学图像等多个数据集和多类架构上的实验表明，所提度量与对抗脆弱性高度相关。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为可解释的诊断工具，补充基于攻击的评估方法，揭示架构敏感性并指导更鲁棒模型的设计。

Abstract: The robustness of deep neural networks is crucial for safety-critical deployments, yet existing evaluation methods are often attack-dependent and lack interpretability. We propose a principled, attack-agnostic robustness metric based on the spectral norm of the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM), which quantifies the worst-case sensitivity of the model's output distribution to input perturbations. Theoretically, we establish that the FIM equals the variance of the input Jacobian and derive closed-form spectral bounds for common architectures, including VGG, ResNet, DenseNet, and Transformer, providing the first theoretical robustness ranking. To enable scalable evaluation, we develop efficient algorithms, including power iteration and Hutchinson-based estimation, that support both white-box and black-box settings. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, including CIFAR, ImageNet, and medical images, and across multiple architectures show a strong correlation between our metric and adversarial vulnerability. Our framework serves as an interpretable diagnostic tool that complements attack-based evaluations, offering insights into architectural sensitivity and guiding the design of more robust models. Code is available at: https://github.com/franz-chang/SRP/.

</details>


### [155] [UniFair: A unified fair clustering approach based on separation and compactness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04777)
*Antonia Karra,Vasiliki Papanikou,Georgios Vardakas,Evaggelia Pitoura,Aristidis Likas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniFair 是一个统一的公平聚类框架，同时优化分离公平性（让保护群体远离决策边界）和社会公平性（减少组内聚类成本差异），在表格和图像数据集上以少量聚类损失换取更低的群体差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平聚类方法通常只优化单一公平性指标，且忽略了聚类成本与决策边界几何结构之间的相互作用。

Method: 提出 UniFair 框架，联合优化分离公平性和社会公平性；开发基于梯度的优化方法用于分离公平和统一的 k-means 目标，并通过自编码器潜空间约束将其扩展到深度聚类。

Result: 在表格和图像数据集上，UniFair 能有效降低边界相关的和成本相关的群体差异，且聚类损失增加较小。

Conclusion: UniFair 通过统一两种公平性指标，提供了一种兼顾分组边界公平和聚类成本公平的聚类方法，且扩展性强。

Abstract: Clustering is increasingly used to support high-impact decisions, yet standard objectives such as $k$-means can produce clusterings that treat demographic groups unequally. Existing fair clustering methods typically optimize a single notion of fairness and often overlook how clustering costs interact with the geometry of the induced decision boundaries. We propose \textsc{UniFair}, a unified framework that jointly optimizes \emph{separation fairness} and \emph{social fairness}. Separation fairness encourages protected groups to lie farther from the induced decision boundaries, while social fairness reduces disparities in within-cluster distortion by penalizing group-wise clustering costs. We develop gradient-based optimization procedures for separation-fair and unified $k$-means objectives, and extend them to deep clustering by enforcing the same criteria in the latent space of an autoencoder. Experiments on tabular and image datasets show that \textsc{UniFair} reduces both boundary-related and cost-based group disparities with only a modest increase in clustering loss.

</details>


### [156] [Uncertainty-Aware (Un)Supervised Few-Shot User Adaptation for On-Device Personalized Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04798)
*Maximilian Burzer,Till Riedel,Michael Beigl,Tobias Röddiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度的原型网络框架，通过闭形式贝叶斯原型估计实现预训练HAR分类器的轻量级个性化，仅需3秒校准数据即可显著提升跨用户活动识别性能（监督+2.76~33.44，无监督+0.56~32.13个宏F1百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器人体活动识别模型在未见用户上因个体运动模式和传感器位置导致的域偏移而性能退化，需要轻量级、适用于有标签/无标签/无校准数据场景的个性化方法。

Method: 提出无梯度框架，将预训练HAR分类器重新用作原型网络，利用先验原型保持零样本性能并正则化适应。对有标签校准数据采用闭形式贝叶斯原型估计，并将相同原理扩展到无标签校准场景。

Result: 仅需每活动3秒校准数据（单样本），监督适应在四个数据集上提升宏F1 +2.76~+33.44个百分点，无监督适应提升+0.56~+32.13个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架仅需闭形式原型更新即可实现高效鲁棒的设备端个性化，适用于预训练HAR分类器的实际部署。

Abstract: Sensor-based Human Activity Recognition (HAR) models often degrade on unseen users due to domain shifts caused by individual movement patterns and sensor placement. Practical wearable HAR systems therefore require personalization methods that are lightweight, applicable whether calibration data is labeled, unlabeled, or unavailable, and robust under limited calibration. We present a gradient-free framework that repurposes pretrained HAR classifiers as Prototypical Networks using using prior prototypes, which preserve zero-shot performance and regularize adaptation. For labeled calibration, we introduce closed-form Bayesian prototype estimation and extend the same principle to unlabeled calibration. With only 3 seconds of calibration data per activity (one shot), supervised adaptation improves macro-F1 by +2.76 to +33.44 percentage points across four datasets, while unsupervised adaptation improves by +0.56 to +32.13 points. Since adaptation requires only closed-form prototype updates, the framework enables efficient and robust on-device personalization of preexisting HAR classifiers.

</details>


### [157] [The Right Measure for Physics-Constrained Generation: A Co-Area Correction for Posterior-Consistent PDE Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04804)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在PDE逆问题中，将扩散/流匹配生成模型与硬物理约束（投影或引导）结合使用会导致贝叶斯后验采样错误，因为条件作用于零测度流形会引发Borel–Kolmogorov悖论，遗漏了关键的co-area (Fixman) Jacobian因子。作者提出CoCoS方法，通过考虑该因子正确恢复后验分布，在物理满足与正确后验采样之间划清界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法广泛使用生成模型（扩散/流匹配）求解PDE逆问题，通过投影或引导将物理定律作为硬约束施加，并声称采样得到贝叶斯后验。作者发现这种做法实际上采样了错误的分布，因此需要揭示偏差来源并给出修正方案。

Method: 从数学上证明，在零测度流形上施加硬约束进行条件采样存在Borel–Kolmogorov悖论，物理上正确的处理应取小残差噪声极限，该极限包含一个co-area (Fixman) Jacobian因子 det(JJ^⊤)^{-1/2}，而投影法和引导法均静默地忽略了该因子。据此提出CoCoS (Measure-Aware Constrained Sampler)，正确纳入该因子以采样精确的后验分布。

Result: 在受控实验中，忽略该因子导致后验误差达到采样噪声基底的20倍（投影法）、9倍（最小位移投影如PCFM）。标量重新加权无法修正偏差，而CoCoS匹配了黄金标准后验，误差仅与采样噪声相当。

Conclusion: “满足物理约束”不等同于“采样正确后验”。CoCoS为不确定性感知的科学推断提供了有理论依据的修正方案，强调了在科学机器学习中处理零测度条件时需谨慎。

Abstract: Generative models -- diffusion and flow matching -- are increasingly used to solve partial differential equation (PDE) inverse problems, enforcing the governing physics as a \emph{hard constraint} (via projection or guidance) and reporting the resulting samples as a Bayesian posterior with calibrated uncertainty. We show that this widely adopted recipe samples the wrong distribution. Conditioning a generative prior on a hard PDE constraint is conditioning on a measure-zero manifold -- an operation that is intrinsically ambiguous (the Borel--Kolmogorov paradox) and whose physically correct resolution, the small-residual-noise limit, carries a co-area (Fixman) Jacobian factor $[det(JJ^{\top})]^{-1/2}$ that projection- and guidance-based methods silently omit. We make the bias precise, show that it grows with the heterogeneity of the constraint sensitivity, and validate it on controlled problems against an \emph{i.i.d.} ground-truth arbiter. The omitted factor is not a second-order detail: removing it inflates the posterior error to $20\times$ the sampling-noise floor; minimal-displacement projection (as in PCFM) is biased at $9\times$ the floor; and a naive scalar reweighting does not fix it. We introduce \textbf{CoCoS}, a measure-aware constrained sampler that targets the correct co-area posterior, and show that it matches the gold-standard posterior to within sampling noise. Our results imply that ``satisfying the physics'' is not the same as ``sampling the posterior,'' and give a principled correction for uncertainty-aware scientific inference.

</details>


### [158] [Scenario Generation for Risk-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Probably Approximately Safe Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04812)
*Mohit Prashant,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于变分自编码器（VAE）和概率屏障证书的双重优化方法，通过逼近状态空间分布并利用上下界屏障证书之间的非鲁棒区域进行采样，来收紧安全约束的违反概率界限，从而为深度强化学习策略提供更严格的安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略在现实部署中容易受到转移扰动影响，导致未知或不安全行为。现有的概率屏障证书方法在策略对转移不确定性敏感时，难以获得安全约束违反概率的紧致上下界。

Method: 利用变分自编码器（VAE）近似状态空间分布，基于状态的潜在特征构建上下界屏障证书，将问题框架为双重优化问题；下界屏障证书提供更保守的安全区域估计，上界则相对宽松；通过采样位于上下界集合差（即非鲁棒区域）中的状态来训练，以收紧界限。

Result: 实验证明了所提方法能够提供更紧致的概率安全保证界限。

Conclusion: 通过VAE潜在特征和双重优化框架，有效提升了深度强化学习策略安全性验证的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Guaranteeing safety is critical to the deployment of reinforcement learning (RL) agents in the real-world, especially as policies learned using deep RL may demonstrate susceptibility to transition perturbations that result in unknown or unsafe behaviour. A method of policy verification is to construct probabilistic barrier-certificates by sampling policy trajectories with respect to safety constraints, thereby demarcating known safe behaviour from unknown behaviour. Obtaining tight upper and lower bounds on the probability of violation of these constraints may be difficult if the policy is susceptible to transition uncertainty or perturbation that places the agent in insufficiently explored states. To address this, we approximate the distribution of the encountered state-space using a variational autoencoder (VAE) and construct upper and lower-bound barrier-certificates using latent characteristics of states to optimize for regions of known, safe behaviour with high confidence. We frame this in our work as a dual optimization problem where the lower-bound barrier-certificate presents a more conservative estimate of the safe region than the upper-bound barrier-certificate. Sampling states that lie within the set difference of the two during training, i.e. the non-robust region, allows us to tighten the upper and lower bounds to provide sharper probabilistic guarantees on safety. Within our study, we describe the guarantees placed and demonstrate the tightness of our bounds experimentally.

</details>


### [159] [Learning While Acting: A Skill-Enhanced Test-Time Co-Evolution Framework for Online Lifelong Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04815)
*Bo Mao,Jie Zhou,Yutao Yang,Xin Li,Xian Wei,Qin Chen,Xingjiao Wu,Liang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LifeSkill框架，通过两阶段强化学习（验证器引导的技能学习+在线技能内化）实现LLM智能体的在线终身学习，在LifelongAgentBench上提升7个绝对百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习智能体在推理时依赖离散技能或静态参数的经验检索，无法像人类学习者一样持续内化测试时的反馈，限制了其在动态交互环境中的适应能力。

Method: 两阶段强化学习框架：1) Verifier-Guided Skill Learning：通过验证器平均成功率奖励候选技能，解决技能提取缺乏直接监督的问题；2) Online Skill Internalization：在测试时交互中，将技能条件轨迹转化为奖励信号，直接将推理能力内化到模型参数中，避免经验检索带来的上下文膨胀。

Result: 在LifelongAgentBench上，LifeSkill相比现有终身学习智能体基线平均性能提升7个绝对百分点。

Conclusion: 验证器引导的技能学习与在线技能内化相结合，使LLM智能体能够在测试时持续优化参数，有效实现终身学习。

Abstract: Lifelong learning is essential for Large Language Model (LLM) agents operating in dynamic, interactive environments. However, existing lifelong learning agents for long-horizon tasks typically depend on discrete skill or past experiences retrieval with static parameters during inference, which prevents them from continuously internalizing test-time feedback like human learners. To bridge this gap, we propose Skill-enhanced Test-Time Co-Evolution (\texttt{LifeSkill}), a two-stage reinforcement learning framework for Online Lifelong Learning Agents. Specifically, we design Verifier-Guided Skill Learning that addresses the lack of direct supervision for skill extraction by rewarding candidate skills according to the average verifier success of multiple skill-conditioned policy rollouts, encouraging the model to generate skills that are useful for solving tasks rather than merely plausible in text. Furthermore, we introduce Online Skill Internalization, which continuously improves the policy model during test-time interaction by transforming skill-conditioned trajectories into reward signals. This enables the agent to directly internalize reasoning capabilities into its parameters, avoiding the context bloat of experience retrieval. Experiments on LifelongAgentBench show that LifeSkill improves average performance by 7 absolute points by comparing with existing lifelong agent baselines.

</details>


### [160] [GeoMin: Data-Efficient Semi-Supervised RLVR via Geometric Distribution Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04516)
*Guangcheng Zhu,Shenzhi Yang,Haobo Wang,Xing Zheng,Yingfan MA,Xuening Feng,Zhongqi Chen,Kai Tang,Zhengqing Zang,Bowen Song,Weiqiang Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoMin 通过建模全局特征分布来解码正确与错误输出的结构差异，从而建立评估自奖励信号可靠性的稳健先验，在仅使用10%标注数据的情况下超越全监督模型，解决了半监督RLVR中的数据效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督RLVR方法依赖粗粒度的性能启发式，导致大量有价值的未标注实例被低效利用，存在严重的数据效率瓶颈。

Method: GeoMin对标注数据的全局特征分布进行建模，解码正确 rollout 与错误 rollout 之间的结构性差异，以此建立稳健先验来评估自奖励信号的可靠性，从而充分挖掘未标注数据的潜力。

Result: GeoMin在最强基线上提升了+4.1%，且仅使用10%的标注数据即可超越全监督模型，展现了卓越的数据效率。

Conclusion: 通过建模全局特征分布来解决半监督RLVR中的数据效率瓶颈，能够在极低标注成本下达到甚至超越全监督性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) significantly advances LLM reasoning, yet it faces a dilemma: standard supervised scaling is throttled by high annotation costs, while unsupervised alternatives suffer from severe model collapse. Recent semi-supervised RLVR methods address this by using a small labeled set to guide unlabeled data, achieving a promising trade-off between training efficacy and annotation cost. However, they suffer from a severe data-efficiency bottleneck due to the reliance on coarse performance heuristics, leaving a vast majority of valuable instances underutilized. To this end, we propose GeoMin, which models global feature distributions on labeled data to decode the structural discrepancy between correct and incorrect rollouts, thereby establishing a robust prior to assess the reliability of self-reward signals and fully unleash the potential of unlabeled data. Empirically, GeoMin outperforms the strongest baselines by +4.1% and even surpasses fully supervised models with only 10% of the annotations, demonstrating remarkable data efficiency.

</details>


### [161] [Reconciling Causality and Non-Equilibrium Thermodynamics with Hamiltonian Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04822)
*Dario Rancati,Max Welling,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出哈密顿因果模型（HCMs），一种轨迹层面的因果框架，将不可变的运动方程与可干预机制分离，通过干预路径律的差异定义因果效应。HCMs与热力学自然衔接，熵产生作为关键因果可观测量，能够揭示沿系统演化过程中标准平均处理效应无法捕捉的因果信号，从而统一统计因果模型与非平稳热力学的语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理时间现象的因果建模面临沿轨迹的干预、非平稳诱导律、路径依赖效应以及动力学反馈等挑战，这些在标准因果模型中难以处理。

Method: 引入哈密顿因果模型（HCMs），将观测变量与局部环境相互作用，干预作为哈密顿机制的控制；分离不可变的运动方程与可干预机制，将因果效应定义为干预路径律之间的差异。

Result: 熵产生可量化过程的不可逆性，是中心因果可观测量，能从数据中估计并揭示沿系统演化过程中的因果效应，这些效应是标准平均处理效应（端点或累积版本）无法捕捉的。

Conclusion: 因果关系并非两个随机变量之间的原初关系，而是源于热力学箭头的不可逆性；本文统一了统计因果模型与非平稳热力学的语言，为描述物理系统中的因果性提供了新工具。

Abstract: Causal modeling of physical temporal phenomena must handle interventions that act along trajectories, nonstationary induced laws, path-dependent effects, and feedback mediated by dynamics, all challenging in standard causal models. We introduce Hamiltonian Causal Models (HCMs), a trajectory-level framework in which observed variables interact with local environments and interventions act as controls of Hamiltonian mechanisms. HCMs separate immutable equations of motion from intervenable mechanisms and define causal effects as discrepancies between interventional path laws. A key motivation for HCMs is their natural interface with non-equilibrium thermodynamics. Entropy production quantifies the irreversibility of a process and is a central causal observable: it is estimable from data and witnesses causal effects along the system's evolution that are invisible to endpoint and cumulative versions of the standard average treatment effect. As in physics, cause and effect are not primitives of the relation between two random variables but arise from the non-invertibility of the thermodynamic arrow. With this, our paper reconciles the language of statistical causal models and non-stationary thermodynamics, offering new tools to describe causality in a wide range of physical systems.

</details>


### [162] [Signed Dual Attention: Capturing Signed Dependencies in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04833)
*Balthazar Courvoisier,Tristan Cazenave*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Signed Dual Attention，一种无需额外参数即可同时捕获正负依赖关系的注意力机制，通过双消息传递方案在单个共享块内传播支持性和对比性信息，达到双头注意力的表达能力，可无缝集成到现有架构中提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制隐含地假设同质性交互，无法建模时间序列等数据中存在的正负依赖关系，限制了其在某些场景下的表达能力。

Method: 提出Signed Dual Attention，受相关结构启发设计双消息传递方案，在单一共享块内同时传播支持性（正相关）和对比性（负相关）信息，无需额外参数即可达到双头注意力的表达能力。

Result: 该模块可无缝集成到现有架构中，在需要符号关系建模的场景下能带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为构建更具表达力和参数效率的Transformer开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Initially developed for natural language processing, Transformer architectures and attention mechanisms are now central to a wide range of deep learning models, including applications in time series forecasting. A standard attention mechanism, however, implicitly assumes homophilic interactions, limiting its ability to model data with positive and negative dependencies, such as time series. In this work, we introduce the Signed Dual Attention, a novel attention formulation that captures both positive and negative relational patterns without additional parameters. By leveraging a dual message-passing scheme inspired by correlation structures, Signed Dual Attention propagates both supportive and contrastive information within a single shared block, effectively achieving the expressiveness of two head attention without additional parameters. This module can be seamlessly integrated into existing architectures and can yield performance gains in certain situations, requiring signed relational modeling. This approach opens a pathway toward more expressive and parameter-efficient transformers.

</details>


### [163] [Prediction Under Imperfect Compression: A Theory of Approximate MDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04834)
*Qian Li,Xinyu Mao,Shang-Hua Teng,Guangxu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究近似最小描述长度（MDL）原则在顺序预测中的可靠性，证明当近似误差为加性且平衡MDL目标中正则化系数λ≥1时，累积期望平方预测误差有界；当λ<1时过拟合会导致无限误差，且乘性近似下模型选择会失败。研究揭示了加性近似与适当正则化对保证近似MDL预测可靠性的充分必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MDL预测理论依赖精确优化目标函数，但实际机器学习只能近似优化。为了弥合这一理论与实践的差距，本文旨在回答：在何种形式的近似和正则化下，近似MDL仍能保证可靠的顺序预测？

Method: 提出平衡MDL目标（λ·L(模型)+L(数据|模型)）的加性松弛近似框架，采用两种证明技术：对于λ>1使用affinity-telescoping参数论证，对于边界λ=1基于精确静态MDL界的似然比停止论证。同时通过构造反例建立下界。

Result: 1) 对任意加性松弛C且λ≥1，累积期望平方预测误差有限；2) 当0<λ<1时，在可估测度的普适类中可发生过拟合导致无限累积期望误差；3) 在乘性近似下，所有λ>0的正则化区域中模型选择均可能失败。

Conclusion: 加性近似与足够的模型复杂度正则化（λ≥1）是保证近似MDL顺序预测可靠性的充分必要条件。这一结果将经典MDL理论扩展到了实际可行的近似优化场景。

Abstract: Minimum Description Length (MDL) formalizes the principle of Occam's razor by optimizing the total description length: $L(\mathrm{model})+L(\mathrm{data} \ | \ \mathrm{model})$. For sequential prediction, the MDL method repeatedly selects a model with a minimum objective score of the observed prefix for the next step prediction. Classical MDL prediction theory shows that exact optimization of the MDL objective indeed provides a strong compression guarantee that supports reliable prediction. However, practical machine learning usually can only find models by approximately optimizing the objective function. To bridge this gap, this paper addresses the following fundamental question: Under what forms of approximation and regularization does approximate MDL still guarantee reliable sequential prediction? This work offers a principled characterization. We prove that for any approximation with additive slack $C$ of the more general form of the balanced MDL objective: $λ\cdot L(\mathrm{model})+L(\mathrm{data} \ | \ \mathrm{model})$, the cumulative expected squared prediction error is finite for all $λ\ge1$. The case $λ>1$ is proved by an affinity-telescoping argument, while the boundary case $λ=1$ is proved by a likelihood-ratio stopping argument based on exact static MDL bounds. Our results establish that classical MDL regularization remains robust to any fixed additive optimization error. Furthermore, we establish that our characterization of the approximate MDL framework is sharp: When $0<λ<1$, overfits can happen to incur infinite cumulative expected error in the universal class of estimable measures, and hence a strong form of model-complexity regularization is necessary. In addition, model selection may fail in every regularized regime $λ>0$, under multiplicative approximation, and thus, additive approximation is both sufficient and essential.

</details>


### [164] [Rethinking Incompleteness: Formalizing Protocol Divergence and Train-Once Learning for Robust IMVC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04857)
*Haolu Liu,Xiyue Wang,Xuanting Xie,Liangjian Wen,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现标准不完全多视图聚类（IMVC）评估中，缺失率相同的协议在完整样本比例上可相差高达50倍，导致学习效果截然不同。作者形式化定义了这一“不完全性散度”现象，并理论证明当完整样本比例低于阈值时，基于重构的目标函数会结构性地失效。为解决该问题，提出CRAFT（完整数据鲁棒注意力掩码融合Transformer），通过逐样本独立性和掩码感知变长融合两个关键特性，使单个模型在完整数据上训练一次即可泛化到各种缺失模式，无需重训练。在七个基准上，CRAFT匹配或超越基线性能，同时训练开销降低8.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准IMVC评估为不同缺失数据配置分别重训练模型，掩盖了一个根本性漏洞：缺失率单独不足以刻画数据不完全性。相同缺失率的协议在完整样本比例上可相差50倍，导致截然不同的学习场景。

Method: 形式化定义不完全性散度，提供衡量缺失数据协议间结构差异的指标；理论证明完整样本比例低于临界阈值时重构目标函数结构性地病态；提出CRAFT，核心设计：(i)逐样本独立性，消除对完整样本共现的依赖，(ii)掩码感知变长融合，通过注意力掩码仅聚合观测视图。单模型在完整数据上训练一次，推理时无需重训练即可泛化到不同缺失模式。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上，CRAFT匹配或超越每个缺失配置独立训练的基线方法，同时训练开销降低8.8倍。

Conclusion: 对缺失数据的鲁棒性可以作为架构的固有属性来实现，而非依赖损失函数设计。CRAFT证明了一个模型一次训练即可处理多种缺失模式的可能性。

Abstract: Standard IMVC evaluation retrains separate models for different missing-data configurations. We show that this paradigm obscures a fundamental vulnerability: missing rate alone is insufficient to characterize data incompleteness. Specifically, we show that protocols with identical nominal missing rates can differ by up to $50\times$ in their proportion of fully observed samples, inducing drastically different learning regimes. We formalize this phenomenon as incompleteness divergence, providing measures that capture structural disparities across missing-data protocols. We further prove that for a broad class of reconstruction-based objectives, learning becomes structurally ill-posed when the proportion of complete samples falls below a critical threshold, leading to near-random performance. To bypass this theoretical bound, we propose CRAFT (Complete-data Robust Attention-masked Fusion Transformer). CRAFT shifts the burden of robustness from the loss function to the architecture via two key properties: (i) per-sample independence, which removes reliance on complete-sample co-occurrence, and (ii) mask-aware variable-length fusion, which aggregates only observed views through attention masking. This design allows a single model, trained once on complete data, to generalize to diverse missing patterns at inference time without retraining. Extensive experiments on seven benchmarks show that CRAFT matches or outperforms per-configuration baselines while reducing training overhead by $8.8\times$, demonstrating that robustness to missing data can be achieved as an inherent architectural property. Code (CRAFT) and our imvc-audit toolkit are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CRAFT-BF80/ and https://anonymous.4open.science/r/imvc-audit-8263/.

</details>


### [165] [Learning Empirically Admissible Neural Heuristics for Combinatorial Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04860)
*Siddharth Sahay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用框架，通过可容许的Bellman算子、非对称损失函数和事后校准偏置来学习验证校准的可容许神经启发函数，在多个组合谜题中实现了无违背可容许性、保持路径最优性并显著减少搜索节点扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的均方误差训练深度强化学习值网络经常产生过高估计，违反可容许性条件，从而损害启发式搜索算法（如A*）的路径最优性保证。

Method: 提出三个关键组件：(1) 低估性可容许Bellman算子用于值网络训练；(2) 非对称损失函数惩罚过高估计；(3) 在验证集上计算事后校准安全偏置以补偿残差神经网络逼近误差。

Result: 在评估协议下观测到无违反可容许性，保持路径最优性；搜索节点扩展减少：2×2 Rubik's Cube 83.0%，3×3 Lights Out 19.9%，8-Puzzle 1.9%。

Conclusion: 提出的校准神经可容许启发式能够在实践中保持路径最优性的同时，显著提升搜索效率，是一种通用且有效的组合谜题求解框架。

Abstract: Finding optimal solution paths for combinatorial puzzles like the Rubik's Cube, sliding tile puzzles, and Lights Out remains a classical challenge in artificial intelligence. Heuristic search algorithms, such as A* , guarantee path optimality only when using an admissible heuristic-one that never overestimates the true remaining cost-to-go. Deep reinforcement learning (RL) methods like DeepCubeA train deep neural networks to approximate cost-to-go heuristics. However, standard mean-squared error (MSE) training regularly yields overestimations, violating admissibility and compromising solution optimality. In this paper, we introduce a generalizable framework for learning validation-calibrated admissible neural heuristics. We train a value network using an underestimating Admissible Bellman Operator combined with an Asymmetric Loss function to penalize overestimation. To account for residual neural function approximation errors, we propose a post-hoc calibration safety offset computed over validation scrambles. We demonstrate that our calibrated neural heuristics achieve no observed admissibility violations under the evaluation protocol and preserve path optimality in practice while reducing search node expansions by up to 83.0% on a 2 by 2 Rubik's Cube, 19.9% on a 3 by 3 Lights Out grid, and 1.9% on an 8-Puzzle compared to standard analytical baselines.

</details>


### [166] [Provably Reduced Sample Cost in Prior-Guided Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04866)
*Leona Hennig,Jasmin Brandt,Lukas Fehring,Barbara Hammer,Marius Lindauer,Marcel Wever*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从固定预算最佳臂识别（fixed-budget best-arm identification）的理论视角，为带先验的多保真度超参数优化（HPO）提供了分布依赖的样本复杂度界，证明信息性先验可显著减少评估预算（实验最高减少90%），为绿色AutoML奠定了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模超参数优化消耗大量计算资源，现有方法虽启发式地利用先验信息加速搜索，但缺乏对先验信息量如何定量降低样本复杂度的理论刻画。

Method: 通过将先验直接建模在臂均值（即配置性能）上，基于固定预算最佳臂识别框架，推导出分布依赖的误差界，定量刻画先验与评估预算之间的关系。

Result: 信息性先验（将概率质量集中在近优臂上）可减少所需评估次数；无信息或误导性先验则恢复基线性能。在合成基准和LCBench上实验验证，最多可实现90%的预算削减且不损失解质量。

Conclusion: 本文为首个为带先验的多保真度HPO提供严格理论分析的工作，为计算高效的绿色AutoML奠定了原则性基础。

Abstract: Large-scale hyperparameter optimization (HPO) in automated machine learning (AutoML) consumes substantial computational resources, raising growing concerns about scalability and energy efficiency. Existing methods use prior information heuristically to accelerate both black-box and multi-fidelity settings, but they lack a characterization of how prior informativeness quantitatively reduces sample complexity. In this work, we provide the first distribution-dependent sample complexity bounds for multi-fidelity HPO with priors through the formal lens of fixed-budget best-arm identification. By modeling priors directly over arm means as configuration performance, we derive explicit, distribution-dependent error bounds that quantify the relationship between priors and evaluation budget. Our analysis shows that informative priors, which concentrate probability mass on near-optimal arms, yield reductions in the number of required evaluations, whereas baseline performance is recovered with uninformative or misleading priors. We conduct proof-of-concept experiments on a synthetic benchmark and on LCBench, a common multi-fidelity HPO benchmark for deep learning, to confirm our theoretical results, achieving up to 90% budget reduction while retaining solution quality. Together, our results provide a principled foundation for prior-guided and compute-efficient green AutoML.

</details>


### [167] [Towards Pretraining Text Encoders for TabPFN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04876)
*Mustafa Tajjar,Alexander Pfefferle,Lennart Purucker,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabPFN Text Adapter，通过轻量级适配器将文本嵌入直接映射为TabPFN的token序列，替代传统PCA压缩流程，在冻结文本编码器和TabPFN的情况下仅训练适配器，消除了信息瓶颈并保持数值性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流程使用PCA压缩文本嵌入再输入TabPFN会造成信息瓶颈（丢弃大部分嵌入维度），而端到端方法需要大量文本表格预训练数据且性能不如TabPFN。受模态对齐方法（LLaVA、TableGPT）启发，希望在不破坏TabPFN预训练能力的前提下高效集成文本特征。

Method: 冻结句子编码器和TabPFN，仅训练一个轻量级文本适配器（text-to-TFM token projection），将文本嵌入映射为TabPFN嵌入空间中的短token序列，替代PCA压缩步骤。

Result: 消除了PCA造成的信息瓶颈，保留了TabPFN在数值数据上的优势，训练效率高于端到端文本-表格联合训练方案。

Conclusion: 提出的TabPFN Text Adapter是一种高效、轻量的文本特征集成方法，在不重新训练大模型的前提下解决了高基数文本特征的处理问题。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, such as TabPFN, achieve strong performance on tabular datasets with numerical and categorical data, but do not natively handle high-cardinality text features. Standard pipelines, therefore, embed text with a language model and compress the resulting vectors with PCA into a small number of scalar features before inputting them into TabPFN. This creates an information bottleneck: most embedding dimensions are discarded, and the compressed representation must then be expanded again by TabPFN's feature encoder. End-to-end alternatives can avoid PCA, but they require large amounts of pretraining data containing text cells and usually perform subpar compared to tabular foundation models that were pretrained on large amounts of synthetic data. Inspired by modality-alignment approaches like LLaVA (vision-to-LLM token projection) and TableGPT-style systems (table-to-LLM token projection), we introduce the TabPFN Text Adapter (text-to-TFM token projection). We freeze both the sentence encoder and TabPFN, and train only a lightweight adapter that maps text embeddings into a short sequence of tokens in TabPFN's embedding space. This design removes the PCA bottleneck, preserves TabPFN's numerical strengths, and is more efficient to train than end-to-end text-tabular pipelines.

</details>


### [168] [Toward Multi-Domain and Long-Tailed Quantization via Feature Alignment and Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04920)
*Chin-Yuan Yeh,Ting-An Chen,De-Nian Yang,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EmaQ和EmaQ-LT，分别解决多域分布偏移和长尾分布下的低比特量化问题，通过CDF投影对齐、敏感度加权聚合、类别条件方差缩放和置信度logit调整实现高效量化，理论保证收敛性，实验验证了在多个基准上的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法主要针对单域和类别平衡数据设计，未充分探索实际场景中存在的域偏移和严重类别不平衡问题。

Method: 1) EmaQ：基于CDF投影对齐域分布 + 敏感度感知权重聚合以稳定多域量化；2) EmaQ-LT：在EmaQ基础上引入类别条件方差缩放和置信度logit调整，缓解头部类别过置信问题。

Result: 在Office-31、Digits（多域）以及SynDigits-LT、CIFAR-10/100-LT（长尾）等基准上，EmaQ和EmaQ-LT在低比特条件下均取得了强性能。

Conclusion: 提出的方法有效填补了多域和长尾场景下量化研究的空白，理论分析和实验共同验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quantizing deep neural networks is essential for efficient inference on resource-constrained devices. However, most existing methods are designed for single-domain and class-balanced data, leaving practical settings with domain shifts or severe class imbalance underexplored. We address these challenges with Efficient Multi-Domain Alignment Quantization (EmaQ), which aligns domain distributions through a CDF-based projection and uses sensitivity-aware weight aggregation to stabilize multi-domain quantization. We further extend EmaQ to EmaQ-LT for long-tailed quantization by introducing class-conditioned variance scaling and confidence-based logit adjustment to mitigate majority-class overconfidence. Theoretical analyses establish convergence guarantees and motivate the proposed sensitivity and scaling mechanisms. Experiments on standard, multi-domain (Office-31, Digits), and long-tailed (SynDigits-LT, CIFAR-10-LT, CIFAR-100-LT) benchmarks show that EmaQ and EmaQ-LT achieve strong low-bit performance under domain shift and class imbalance.

</details>


### [169] [Reproducing, Analyzing, and Detecting Reward Hacking in Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04923)
*Xuekang Wang,Zhuoyuan Hao,Shuo Hou,Hao Peng,Juanzi Li,Xiaozhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHERRL是一个可控的奖励黑客环境，通过向LLM裁判注入已知偏见，稳定复现基于规则强化学习中的奖励黑客行为，提供研究其机制和缓解方法的实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的强化学习中使用LLM作为裁判打分时，策略模型可能利用裁判的潜在偏见进行奖励黑客，导致无效或不安全的训练结果。实际中这类黑客行为微妙且与多种偏见交织，难以分析、检测和缓解。

Method: 提出CHERRL环境，通过向LLM裁判注入已知偏见，实现奖励黑客的稳定复现、奖励偏离的显式观察以及黑客起始时间的精确识别，从而提供干净的实验测试床。

Result: 从可发现性和可利用性角度分析了不同裁判偏见，并探索了基于智能体的系统用于自动检测训练日志中的奖励黑客起始时间。

Conclusion: CHERRL为研究基于规则的强化学习中奖励黑客的机制和缓解方法提供了有效工具，代码和实验环境已公开。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) uses an LLM-as-a-Judge (LaaJ) to score model outputs according to rubrics as rewards. However, policy models may exploit latent biases in the judge, leading to reward hacking and ineffective or unsafe training outcomes. In real-world rubric-based RL, such hacking behaviors are often subtle and entangled with multiple judge biases, making them difficult to analyze, detect, and mitigate. In this paper, we introduce CHERRL, a controllable hacking environment for rubric-based RL. By injecting known biases into LaaJ, CHERRL enables stable reproduction of reward hacking, explicit observation of reward divergence, and precise identification of hacking onset. This provides a clean experimental testbed for studying the mechanisms and mitigations of reward hacking in rubric-based RL. To demonstrate its utility, we analyze different judge biases from the perspectives of discoverability and exploitability, and explore an agent-based system for automatically detecting reward hacking onset from training logs. The code and environment are publicly available at https://github.com/THUAIS-Lab/CHERRL.

</details>


### [170] [Data Attribution in Large Language Models via Bidirectional Gradient Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04928)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Kaan Bayraktar,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双向梯度优化的训练数据归因方法，通过扰动基础模型（对生成文本样本进行梯度上升和下降）并测量训练样本上的损失变化，实现了对自回归LLMs输出与训练数据之间因果关系的追溯，支持任意粒度的事实和风格归因，在预训练模型上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在多样化应用中广泛部署，但理解哪些训练数据最影响模型输出仍是一个基本开放问题，这对治理、问责和数据溯源至关重要。

Method: 扩展逆问题公式：若模型在训练时见过生成输出，训练数据会如何变化。通过双向梯度优化（梯度上升和下降）扰动基础模型，测量训练样本上的损失变化，支持任意数据粒度的归因。

Result: 在已知数据集的预训练模型上评估，该方法在影响指标上优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 该方法增强了模型可解释性，是构建可信AI系统的必要条件。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across diverse applications, raising critical questions for governance, accountability, and data provenance. Understanding which training data most influenced a model's output remains a fundamental open problem. We address this challenge through training data attribution (TDA) for auto-regressive LLMs by expanding upon the inverse formulation: How would training data be affected if the model had seen the generated output during training? Our method perturbs the base model using bidirectional gradient optimization (gradient ascent and descent) on a generated text sample and measures the resulting change in loss across training samples. Our framework supports attribution at arbitrary data granularity, enabling both factual and stylistic attribution. We evaluate our method against baselines on pretrained models with known datasets, and show that it outperforms previous work on influence metrics, thereby enhancing model interpretability, an essential requirement for accountable AI systems.

</details>


### [171] [Sequential Data Poisoning in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04929)
*Jack Sanderson,Yihan Wang,Xiaoqian Lu,Gautam Kamath,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"顺序数据投毒"威胁模型，揭示在LLM后训练流水线（SFT→DPO/PPO）中，多个攻击者分别投毒不同阶段的数据集时，会产生"单一攻击者幻觉"：单独评估每个攻击者威胁极小，但多阶段协作投毒则暴露真实漏洞。SFT→DPO中贡献是加性的，SFT→PPO中是互补的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献假设数据投毒攻击可能发生在每个训练阶段，但忽略了存在多个攻击者的可能性。为了研究整个后训练流水线的可信赖性，作者提出了顺序数据投毒威胁模型。

Method: 提出顺序数据投毒威胁模型，多个攻击者分别投毒SFT数据集和偏好数据集。在SFT→DPO和SFT→PPO两种流水线中，系统性地研究多阶段协作投毒的效果，并与单一阶段投毒进行对比分析。

Result: 发现"单一攻击者幻觉"：每个攻击者单独评估时威胁可忽略，但协作投毒时漏洞显著暴露。SFT→DPO中投毒预算跨阶段分配优于集中单一阶段（加性效应）；SFT→PPO中单独SFT或奖励模型投毒均无效，但组合投毒成功（互补效应）。

Conclusion: 安全分析中单独评估每个训练阶段会系统性低估仅从多阶段交互中产生的复合漏洞，强调了在后训练流水线中跨阶段整体评估安全性的必要性。

Abstract: LLM post-training proceeds through multiple stages, e.g., supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) or direct preference optimization (DPO), where each stage draws data from different, potentially untrusted sources. Existing literature assumes data poisoning attacks may occur at each training stage, but neglects the possibility of multiple attackers. To study the trustworthiness of the entire post-training pipeline, we propose the threat model of sequential data poisoning, where multiple adversaries separately poison the SFT and preference datasets. Under this threat model, we identify the single-attacker illusion: each adversary, evaluated in isolation, appears to pose a negligible threat. Yet when adversaries collaborate across stages, the true vulnerability is revealed. In the SFT $\to$ DPO pipeline, their contributions are additive: splitting a fixed poison budget across stages outperforms concentrating it in either stage alone. In the SFT $\to$ PPO pipeline, their contributions are complementary: neither SFT nor reward model poisoning succeeds individually, yet their combination does. These findings show that security analyses of individual post-training stages systematically underestimate compound vulnerabilities that emerge only from their interaction. Code is available at https://github.com/jcksanderson/sequential-poisoning.

</details>


### [172] [Mean-based algorithms: A lower bound and regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04931)
*Julius Durmann,Amelie Kleber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在未知时间步长和仅提供赌博机反馈条件下的均值算法，给出了算法定义序列γ_t的下界，提出了两种改进的均值算法（泛化ε-greedy和扩展Exp3），实验表明均值算法虽略慢但具有竞争力，并揭示了均值算法与无遗憾算法之间存在非平凡的交集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明均值算法在博弈中能收敛到序列占优动作并逼近纳什均衡，但在赌博机反馈场景下收敛速度较慢。本文旨在理论上确定均值算法的学习极限，并在未知时间步长条件下设计更有效的均值算法。

Method: 通过理论推导给出算法定义序列γ_t的第一个下界；提出两种均值算法：一是泛化ε-greedy的方法，二是将均值Exp3扩展到未知时间步长；通过实验对比均值算法与其他赌博机反馈算法的性能；分析均值算法与无遗憾算法之间的关系。

Result: 给出了均值算法在未知时间步长下γ_t的下界；提出的两种算法在实验中表现略慢但具有竞争力；证明了存在同时满足均值属性和无遗憾属性的算法，揭示了二者交集的非平凡性。

Conclusion: 均值算法在未知时间步长场景下存在理论上的学习极限，但通过合理设计仍可与其他赌博机反馈算法竞争；均值算法与无遗憾算法并非互斥，丰富了对此类算法"可剥削性"的理解。

Abstract: Mean-based algorithms are a class of online learning algorithms that assign low probability to actions with low average rewards. Recent work indicates these algorithms converge favorably to serially undominated actions, which approximate Nash equilibria in economic games. However, empirical studies also show slower convergence compared to established algorithms in bandit-feedback scenarios.
  We study mean-based algorithms when the time horizon is unknown and only bandit feedback is available. In this setting, we provide the first lower bound on the algorithm-defining sequence $γ_t$ that formally establishes a limit on how fast these algorithms can learn. Additionally, we propose two mean-based algorithms: one generalizes $ε$-greedy, and the other extends the mean-based Exp3 to unknown horizons. Our experiments show that mean-based algorithms, although slightly slower, can perform competitively with other bandit-feedback algorithms.
  We further analyze the relationship to no-regret algorithms. Depending on the choice of $γ_t$, the intersection with no-regret algorithms is non-trivial, and we show that algorithms exist that are both mean-based and no-regret. This adds context to the "exploitability" of this class of algorithms that previous contributions suggest.

</details>


### [173] [STaR-Quant: State-Time Consistent Post-Training Quantization for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04945)
*Xin Yan,Aqiang Wang,Zhenglin Wan,Xingrui Yuand Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型（DLLM）的低比特量化难题，本文提出STaR-Quant框架，通过状态引导激活变换（SGAT）和时序注意力补偿（TAC）分别解决状态相关激活差异和时序误差累积问题，在保持性能的同时实现最高1.69倍加速和3.14倍内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（DLLM）虽以迭代掩码去噪和双向上下文生成成为自回归模型的替代方案，但其庞大模型规模和迭代去噪过程带来了巨大的内存和计算开销，亟需高效部署的后训练量化（PTQ）方法。

Method: 提出STaR-Quant框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 状态引导激活变换（SGAT），将掩码与未掩码token分配至不同的激活变换空间，并通过统一静态权值侧变换实现；(2) 时序注意力补偿（TAC），利用轻量级块对角仿射映射校正量化后的注意力表示，缓解跨步误差累积。

Result: 在代表性DLLM上的实验表明，STaR-Quant在低比特权值-激活量化任务中一致优于强PTQ基线，相比FP16部署实现最高1.69倍加速和3.14倍内存节省。

Conclusion: STaR-Quant通过状态-时间一致性设计，有效解决了DLLM量化中的激活分布差异和时序误差累积两大关键挑战，实现了高效部署。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (DLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs by generating text through iterative masked denoising with bidirectional context. However, their large model sizes and iterative denoising process introduce substantial memory and computational overhead, motivating post-training quantization for efficient deployment. In this paper, we identify two key challenges for low-bit DLLM quantization: state-dependent activation disparity and temporal error accumulation. Masked and unmasked tokens exhibit different activation distributions within each denoising step, while quantization errors can accumulate across steps during iterative decoding. To address these challenges, we propose STaR-Quant, a state-time consistent PTQ framework for DLLMs. STaR-Quant introduces State-Guided Activation Transformation (SGAT) to assign masked and unmasked tokens to different activation transformation spaces with a unified static weight-side transformation. It further introduces Temporal Attention Compensation (TAC) to correct the quantized attention representation via a lightweight block-diagonal affine mapping. Experiments on representative DLLMs demonstrate that STaR-Quant consistently improves low-bit weight-activation quantization over strong PTQ baselines, while delivering up to 1.69x speedup and 3.14x memory saving over FP16 deployment.

</details>


### [174] [Be Fair! Can Machine Learning Engineering Agents Adhere to Fairness Constraints?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04971)
*Anna Richter,Julia Stoyanovich,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有机器学习工程（MLE）智能体在敏感领域存在责任鸿沟，现有基准不足以评估其安全性。通过黑色素瘤分类的探索性实验发现，即使使用公平性导向提示，MLE智能体生成的流水线在预测质量和公平性上均持续低于手动基线，并表现出高方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLE智能体基准忽视了在敏感和受监管领域中，端到端自动化带来的责任鸿沟问题——终端用户（尤其是非技术领域专家）无法感知影响正确性、鲁棒性、公平性和合规性的设计选择。

Method: 提出面向责任的评估框架应满足的期望标准，并以黑色素瘤分类任务为案例，以肤色间公平性为责任约束，评估两个近期MLE智能体。

Result: MLE智能体生成的流水线在预测质量和公平性上均持续低于手动设计的基线，且表现出高方差；即便使用公平性导向提示，这一问题仍未解决。

Conclusion: 需要进一步研究重新设计MLE智能体，使人类能够引导搜索过程并可靠评估生成流水线的合规性与质量。

Abstract: Machine learning engineering (MLE) agents promise to automate end-to-end ML pipeline development from raw data and natural language instructions, potentially making ML accessible to non-technical domain experts. However, in sensitive and regulated domains, this abstraction creates a responsibility gap: end-users may lack visibility into design choices that affect correctness, robustness, fairness, and regulatory compliance. We argue that existing benchmarks are insufficient to assess whether MLE agents can be safely applied in such settings. We propose desiderata for a responsibility-centered evaluation framework and conduct an exploratory study on melanoma classification, focusing on fairness across skin tones as a responsibility constraint. When evaluating two recent MLE agents, we find that agent-generated pipelines show high variance and consistently underperform manually designed baselines in both predictive quality and fairness, despite fairness-oriented prompts. These preliminary results suggest that further research is needed towards redesigning MLE agents to allow humans to guide the search process and reliably assess the compliance and quality of the generated ML pipelines.

</details>


### [175] [Invariant Gradient Alignment for Robust Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05025)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不变梯度对齐（IGA）框架，通过逻辑同构集、连续梯度冲突掩码和截断SVD投影解决LLM的捷径学习问题，在分布外泛化上显著优于ERM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在捷径学习问题，在语义表面不同但逻辑结构相同的分布外输入上系统性地失败，这损害了知识蒸馏流程中思维链推理的迁移效果。

Method: IGA框架包含三个创新：(i) 逻辑同构集——在数学、医学、法律、科学等不同语义域中共享相同逻辑结构的问题组；(ii) 可微分的连续梯度冲突掩码——抑制跨域梯度方差高的参数维度，保留不变方向；(iii) 截断SVD投影——将掩码后的梯度投影回LoRA低秩流形，保持参数效率。

Result: 在四个基准上优于八种基线，准确率相比ERM-SFT提升高达14.3个百分点；逻辑一致性得分0.031 vs 0.142，表示不变性提升四倍。

Conclusion: IGA在理论上比ERM有更紧的OOD泛化界，其界随同构域数量缩放，并在温和正则条件下以标准SGD速率收敛。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) suffer from shortcut learning: they systematically fail on out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs whose semantic surface differs from training data, even when the logical structure is identical. This undermines knowledge distillation pipelines that transfer chain-of-thought reasoning to smaller students. We introduce Invariant Gradient Alignment (IGA), a training framework that aligns gradient updates across semantically diverse but logically isomorphic examples via three innovations: (i) Logical Isomer Sets, groups of problems sharing identical logical structure across distinct semantic domains (mathematics, medicine, law, science); (ii) a differentiable \emph{Continuous Gradient Conflict Mask}, that suppresses parameter dimensions with high cross-domain gradient variance while preserving invariant directions; and (iii) a truncated SVD projection of the masked gradient back onto the LoRA low-rank manifold, maintaining parameter efficiency throughout. Theoretically, IGA yields tighter OOD generalization bounds than ERM, scaling with the number of isomer domains, and converges at the standard SGD rate under mild regularity. Empirically, IGA outperforms eight baselines across four benchmarks with accuracy gains up to 14.3 pp over ERM-SFT and a Logical Consistency Score of 0.031 versus 0.142 -- a fourfold improvement in representational invariance.

</details>


### [176] [AlphaQ: Calibration-Free Bit Allocation for Mixture-of-Experts Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04980)
*Wanqi Yang,Yuexiao Ma,Alexander Conzelmann,Xiawu Zheng,Michael W. Mahoney,T. Konstantin Rusch,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlphaQ提出了一种无需校准数据的混合精度量化方法，通过专家权重谱的厚尾性来决定位宽分配，在MoE大模型上实现了平均3.5比特下的近全精度性能与4倍以上内存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE量化方法依赖校准数据估计专家重要性，但前沿MoE大模型的训练数据是专有的、不可获取的，校准集作为代理分布会误估专家利用率，导致次优的位分配。作者希望通过无需数据的方法解决这一矛盾。

Method: 基于重尾自正则化(HT-SR)理论，测量每个专家权重矩阵的谱厚尾性（heavy-tailedness），认为谱越厚尾的专家训练越充分，应分配更高位宽；反之可更激进量化。通过求解预算约束下的优化问题，最小化总量化误差。

Result: 在多个MoE模型上，AlphaQ在同等比特预算下持续优于基于校准数据的基线方法。在Qwen1.5-MoE上，平均3.5比特精度即达到近全精度性能，并实现超过4倍内存压缩。

Conclusion: 无需校准数据的厚尾性驱动的位分配方法在MoE量化中是有效且实用的，尤其适用于训练数据不可知的前沿模型。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale model capacity through sparse expert activation, but their deployment remains memory-bound because all expert weights must reside in memory. Mixed-precision quantization can substantially reduce this footprint by assigning different bit-widths to different experts. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on calibration data to estimate expert importance and determine bit allocation. For frontier MoE LLMs, the original training data, and hence the true training distribution, is proprietary and inaccessible. As a result, calibration sets are inevitably imperfect surrogates, and this can misestimate expert utilization and lead to suboptimal bit allocation. Motivated by the substantial cross-expert quality variability observed in modern MoE models, and by the success of Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory at predicting neural network model quality without access to training or testing data, we propose AlphaQ, a calibration-free bit-allocation method for MoE quantization. AlphaQ draws on HT-SR theory and follows a simple principle: experts with more heavy-tailed weight spectra are typically better trained and hence should receive higher bit-widths, while experts with weaker heavy-tailed structure can be quantized more aggressively. AlphaQ operationalizes this principle by measuring expert-wise spectral heavy-tailedness and solving a budget-constrained optimization problem that minimizes total quantization error under a global bit-budget constraint. Across several MoE models, AlphaQ consistently outperforms calibration-based baselines under matched bit budgets. Notably, on Qwen1.5-MoE, AlphaQ achieves near full-precision accuracy with an average expert precision of only 3.5 bits, while delivering more than 4$\times$ memory compression. Our code is available at https://github.com/Superone77/AlphaQ.

</details>


### [177] [Towards Efficient and Evidence-grounded Mobility Prediction with LLM-Driven Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05130)
*Linyao Chen,Qinlao Zhao,Zechen Li,Mingming Li,Likun Ni,Jinyu Chen,Yuhao Yao,Xuan Song,Noboru Koshizuka,Hiroki Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AgentMob，一种无需训练的LLM驱动智能体框架，通过自适应证据收集将下一位置预测转化为证据控制的决策过程，在三个移动性数据集上达到训练自由LLM方法的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督序列模型准确率高但缺乏决策透明度，而基于LLM的方法多依赖静态提示和单次推理，在移动信号弱或冲突时无法主动寻找额外证据。

Method: 采用双路径机制：常规案例通过基于历史规律性的快速路径直接解决；模糊案例则触发迭代工具使用，收集近期轨迹、历史行为、停留-移动可能性和地理证据等，实现自适应证据控制决策。

Result: 在三个数据集上达到训练自由LLM方法最优性能：GPT-5.4在BW上Acc@1为71.42%，YJMob100K上33.14%，Shanghai ISP上33.50%。在BW非快速路径案例中，LLM控制器将Acc@1从30.65%提升至48.62%。

Conclusion: 无需训练的自适应证据收集策略能有效解决模糊预测场景，在保持可解释性的同时显著提升准确性。

Abstract: Individual-level mobility prediction is central to urban simulation, transportation planning, and policy analysis. Supervised sequence models achieve strong accuracy but require task-specific training and offer limited decision-level transparency. Recent LLM-based methods improve interpretability, yet mostly rely on static prompts and single-pass inference, limiting their ability to seek additional evidence when mobility signals are weak or conflicting. We propose \method{}, a training-free LLM-driven agent framework that formulates next-location prediction as adaptive evidence-controlled decision making. \method{} resolves routine cases through a fast path based on historical regularity, while ambiguous cases trigger iterative tool use over recent trajectories, historical behavior, stay-move likelihood, and geographical evidence. Across three mobility datasets, AgentMob achieves the strongest overall performance among training-free LLM-based methods, with GPT-5.4 reaching 71.42\% Acc@1 on BW, 33.14\% on YJMob100K, and 33.50\% on Shanghai ISP. On BW non-fast-path cases, the LLM controller improves Acc@1 from 30.65\% to 48.62\% over a same-tool statistical baseline, showing that its main benefit lies in resolving ambiguous predictions through adaptive evidence gathering. Our code is available at https://github.com/Unknown-zoo/AgentMob.

</details>


### [178] [New Benchmarking Shows Limited Generalization Power of TCR Antigenic Epitope Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04994)
*Yiming Liao,Yiheng Li,Ning Jiang,Bo Li,Keke Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前TCR-抗原特异性预测模型缺乏足够的敏感性和特异性，主要瓶颈在于缺乏严格定义的未见基准数据集。作者提出了两类互补的基准数据集，为模型评估和下一代算法开发提供框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TCR-抗原特异性预测模型的性能不足以支持广泛应用，主要障碍是缺乏能够公正评估模型泛化能力的基准数据集。

Method: 提出两类互补的、符合严格标准的未见基准数据集，用于无偏评估模型性能。

Result: 两类互补的基准数据集为TCR-抗原特异性预测提供了鲁棒的评估框架。

Conclusion: 这些数据集不仅是模型评估的可靠工具，也为下一代TCR-抗原预测算法的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Accurate computational prediction of T cell receptor (TCR) antigen specificity would transform the study of T cell biology and enable scalable immune engineering, yet existing models lack sufficient sensitivity and specificity for broad applications. A major limitation is the absence of rigorously defined, unseen benchmark datasets that allow unbiased evaluation of model performance and generalizability. Here, we describe two complementary classes of datasets that meet this criterion and argue that they provide both a robust framework for model assessment and a foundation for next-generation TCR-antigen prediction algorithm development.

</details>


### [179] [Failed Reasoning Traces Tell You What Is Fixable (But Not by Reading Them)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05145)
*Nizar Islah,Istabrak Abbes,Irina Rish,Sarath Chandar,Eilif B. Muller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出失败轨迹包含可恢复性结构信息，通过三条轨迹级特征（源自干预结构而非文本内容）将失败聚类为稳定类型，支持无需训练的测试时路由规则，在Steerable-Hard子集上提升+12.2%的救援成功率，跨模型族迁移有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练语言模型在推理失败时通常直接丢弃失败轨迹并重新采样，忽略了失败中蕴含的结构化信息——某些失败源于随机采样不佳（可通过更多重试解决），而另一些是结构性失败（无法通过重试克服）。论文旨在利用失败轨迹的分布特征来区分这两类失败，实现更高效的测试时资源分配。

Method: 提出三条问题级轨迹特征：基于可用干预结构推导，从失败轨迹的分布特征（而非文本内容）中提取。利用这些特征将失败聚类为稳定类型，并构建无需训练的路由规则，决定何时重试、何时使用干预（如提示修改）、何时放弃。

Result: 特征聚类准确率达84.3±4.3%，比多数类基线高20%；路由规则在Steerable-Hard子集上提升救援成功率12.2%；特征和路由规则在两个跨模型族（cross-family）探针上可迁移。

Conclusion: 失败轨迹不应被丢弃，其分布特征可作为诊断对象，支持测试时路由和后训练分析，且无需训练时或权重空间访问。

Abstract: When post-trained language models fail on reasoning problems, the common test-time-scaling response is to spend more compute on additional attempts, and the failed traces play no further role.
