We argue this discards a crucial signal; some failures come from unlucky sampling, where more rollouts help, while others are structural and resist resampling regardless of budget. We propose that failed traces encode recoverability structure: the inference-time signature of which test-time interventions can rescue a given failure. Three problem-level trajectory features, derived from the structure of available interventions, recover this structure from the distributional signature of failed rollouts, not their text. They cluster failures into stable regimes, characterize the failure topography of different post-training methods ($84.3{\pm}4.3\%$ accuracy, $+20\%$ over a majority-class baseline), and support a training-free routing rule that lifts rescue by $+12.2\%$ on the deployment-relevant Steerable-Hard subset (failures where retry is insufficient and a bounded intervention is reachable). The features and the routing rule transfer across two cross-family probes. The same three features thus convert failed traces from discarded data into a diagnostic object, supporting test-time routing and post-training analysis without training-time or weight-space access.

</details>


### [180] [Enhancing the MADDPG Algorithm for Multi-Agent Learning via Action Inference and Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05021)
*Marc Walden,Jason Liu,Shaashwath Sivakumar,Ryan Liu,Hamza Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体深度确定性策略梯度（MADDPG）算法提出了两项改进：动作推理机制和基于几何分布的重要性采样策略，并在PettingZoo库的离散动作捕食者-猎物任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习面临环境非平稳性和智能体间协作困难等挑战，现有MADDPG算法在这些方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 1）提出Action Inference机制，让每个智能体预测其他智能体的意图动作，提升自身策略的准确性和稳定性；2）在经验回放池中采用基于几何分布的重要性采样策略，优先采样更新近、信息量更大的经验以缓解非平稳性。

Result: 在PettingZoo的离散动作捕食者-猎物任务上，Action Inference有效提升了学习稳定性和智能体间协作；几何分布重要性采样显著提升了探索效率。

Conclusion: 两项改进均能有效提升MADDPG在多智能体场景下的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: We investigate multi-agent deep reinforcement learning and propose two enhancements to the Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) algorithm. First, we introduce a novel Action Inference mechanism that enables each agent to predict other agents' intended actions, thereby improving the accuracy and stability of its own policy. Second, we apply an importance sampling strategy, using geometric distribution, in the replay buffer to prioritize more recent and informative experiences, which helps mitigate the non-stationarity inherent in multi-agent environments. We evaluate both modifications on the discrete-action Predator-Prey task provided by the PettingZoo library, a flexible Python interface for general multi-agent reinforcement learning benchmarks. Our results indicate that Action Inference is effective in improving learning stability and inter-agent cooperation and that importance sampling using geometric distribution can lead to significant improvements in exploration efficiency over standard MADDPG. Code available at https://github.com/shaashwathsivakumar/MARL_Proj

</details>


### [181] [Reinforcement Learning from Rich Feedback with Distributional DAgger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05152)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Jacob Fein-Ashley,Paria Rashidinejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DistIL，一种基于前向交叉熵的分布化DAgger算法，利用丰富的反馈信号（如执行轨迹、工具输出等）进行推理模型训练，在理论上保证单调策略改进和遗憾界，并在科学推理、编程和数学问题求解上优于RLVR和自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法仅使用单一比特的最终答案正确性信号，忽略了执行轨迹、工具输出、专家修正和模型自评估等丰富反馈。本文旨在利用这些丰富反馈来提升推理模型训练效果。

Method: 提出分布化DAgger算法，使学习器能够局部访问当前策略访问状态上的专家分布，优化一个简单的前向交叉熵目标函数。该目标支持黑盒专家，其序列级梯度通过将未来的专家-学生分歧传播回早期决策来实现丰富的信用分配。

Result: 理论上前向交叉熵保证单调策略改进和遗憾界；反向KL和Jensen-Shannon散度的自蒸馏目标无法保证单调改进。经验上，DistIL在科学推理、编程和数学问题求解等多个领域优于RLVR和自蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 前向交叉熵目标通过优化教师加权成功可能性的下界，能够有效利用丰富反馈信号，实现更优的Pass@N性能，是当前RLVR范式的有效改进。

Abstract: Reasoning models have advanced rapidly, but the dominant reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) recipe remains surprisingly narrow: sample many responses and reward each with a single bit indicating whether the final answer is correct. Yet many settings provide rich feedback, including execution traces, tool outputs, expert corrections, and model self-evaluations. We study how to use such feedback through a distributional variant of the classic imitation learning algorithm DAgger, where the learner has local access to an expert distribution on states visited by the current policy. This yields a simple forward cross-entropy objective that admits a blackbox expert and whose sequence-level gradient {conduct rich credit assignment by propagating} future expert-student disagreement back to earlier decisions. We show that prior RL with self-distillation objectives based on reverse KL or Jensen-Shannon fail to guarantee monotonic policy improvement: even when the expert has higher reward, their updates may increase probability on worse actions. In contrast, we show that forward cross-entropy admits monotonic policy improvement and enjoys guarantees on regret. We further show that our objective optimizes a lower bound on teacher-weighted likelihood of success, leading to improved Pass@N. Empirically, our approach, DistIL, improves over RLVR and RL with self-distillation baselines across a variety of domains: scientific reasoning, coding, and solving hard mathematical problems.

</details>


### [182] [Validity Threats for Foundation Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05029)
*Gunnar König,Martin Pawelczyk,Ulrike von Luxburg,Sebastian Bordt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在大规模基础模型研究中，受限于计算成本，研究者常采用代理实验、观察性研究和单次运行设计等近似策略来替代理想的控制实验。这些策略虽节省计算资源，却引入了不同类型的效度威胁（统计、内部、外部、构念效度）。作者提出一个将基础模型研究视为因果推断问题的评估框架，系统分析了各策略的效度特征，为研究者提供了审查研究设计效度威胁的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型规模不断增大，传统控制实验的成本已高到难以承受，社区转向各种低成本近似研究策略，但这些策略的有效性缺乏系统性评估，其隐含假设被违背时可能导致研究结论无效。

Method: 将基础模型研究建模为因果推断问题，借鉴社会科学的四种效度类型（统计、内部、外部、构念效度），系统评估代理实验、观察性研究和单次运行设计等不同研究策略的效度特征。

Result: 代理实验以牺牲外部和构念效度为代价换取统计和内部效度；观察性研究面临混杂和效应异质性挑战；单次运行设计受处理单元间干扰影响。不同策略展现出差异化的效度特征轮廓。

Conclusion: 基础模型研究中"没有免费的午餐"，计算成本的降低必然伴随着效度威胁的增加。作者提出的评估框架可作为研究者审视研究设计效度威胁的实践工具。

Abstract: Controlled experiments are the backbone of machine learning research, but at the scale of modern foundation models, they have become prohibitively expensive. Instead, the community increasingly relies on research strategies that approximate the ideal experiment at a fraction of the cost: proxy experiments and scaling laws, observational studies with publicly available models, and single-run designs that leverage variation within individual training runs. In this work, we argue that there is no free lunch when approximating large-scale experiments on a compute budget. Specifically, savings in compute come at the cost of validity threats -- hidden and sometimes untestable assumptions that, when violated, can invalidate research claims. To help navigate such threats, we propose an evaluation framework that casts foundation model research as a causal inference problem. Within this framework, we evaluate different research strategies through four types of validity adapted from the empirical social sciences -- statistical, internal, external, and construct validity. We find that each strategy comes with a characteristic validity profile: proxy experiments trade external and construct validity for statistical and internal validity; observational studies face confounding and effect heterogeneity; and single-run designs are strained by interference between treated units. This analysis reveals several validity threats that have received insufficient attention in the literature. Overall, our evaluation framework provides researchers with a practical toolkit for scrutinizing validity threats in foundation model research~designs.

</details>


### [183] [In-Context Graphical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05042)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICG-I，一种自回归图Transformer，通过模仿变量消除并利用张量训练压缩来兼顾离散图模型边缘推理的精确性与可扩展性，在标准实例和受阻自旋玻璃问题上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散图模型的边缘推理存在精确性与可扩展性之间的根本矛盾：精确算法在高树宽图上不可行，迭代近似方法在受阻拓扑上缺乏收敛保证。作者认为这一困境源于归纳偏置的错配——迭代方法放弃了顺序消元结构，而该结构正是精确推理正确性的关键。

Method: 提出In-Context Graphical Inference (ICG-I)，一种自回归图Transformer。核心思路是恢复变量消除的顺序结构：用学习的方式替代传统消元，使用张量训练(Tensor-Train)压缩中间因子；结合Dirichlet输出层和加权共形预测(Weighted Conformal Prediction)以提供校准的、分布自由的覆盖保证。

Result: 在标准实例上将MAE从0.041(最佳基线)降至0.020；在N=500的受阻自旋玻璃问题上，BP完全发散，ICG-I达到0.048 MAE，全面超越所有基线取得SOTA。

Conclusion: TT压缩误差在自回归链中至多线性传播；Dirichlet-Multinomial损失是适当的评分规则；WCP在估计密度比下能保持覆盖保证且退化可量化。ICG-I有效弥合了精确性与可扩展性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Marginal inference in discrete graphical models forces a choice between exactness and scalability: exact algorithms are intractable for high-treewidth graphs, while iterative approximations (Belief Propagation, variational methods) sacrifice convergence guarantees on frustrated topologies. We argue that this dichotomy stems from a mismatched inductive bias: iterative methods abandon the sequential elimination structure that makes exact inference correct. We introduce In-Context Graphical Inference (ICG-I), an autoregressive Graph Transformer that restores this structure by mimicking Variable Elimination with learned, Tensor- Train-compressed intermediate factors, paired with a Dirichlet output layer and Weighted Conformal Prediction for calibrated, distribution-free coverage guarantees under topological shift. We prove that TT compression errors propagate at most lincarly through the autoregressive chain, that the Dirichlet-Multinomial loss is a proper scoring rule, and that WCP maintains coverage with a quantifiable degradation under estimated density ratios. We conducted intensive experiments to evaluate ICG-I and achieved state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks. ICG-I reduces MAE from 0.041 (best baseline) to 0.020 on standard instances and achieves 0.048 on N=500 frustrated spin glasses where BP diverges entirely.

</details>


### [184] [FLAGG: Flexible Autoregressive Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05067)
*Samuel Cognolato,Alessandro Sperduti,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLAGG框架通过将一次性图生成模型转化为自回归形式，结合两者优势，在不同规模和领域的图数据集上优于纯一次性或纯自回归基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性模型和自回归模型各自在不同图域表现优异，但都无法普适于所有图类别（一次性模型难以生成大图，自回归模型在小图上表现不佳），因此需要灵活结合两种方法。

Method: 提出FLAGG框架，通过随机节点移除过程指定序贯策略，并利用插入模型学习逆转该过程，使任何一次性模型具备自回归能力，实现图的部分序贯生成。

Result: 使用DiGress一次性模型在多个不同规模和领域的图数据集上评估，FLAGG在采样质量上优于一次性模型和自回归基线方法。

Conclusion: FLAGG框架有效融合了一次性生成与自回归生成的优点，是一种灵活且通用的图生成方案。

Abstract: The Deep Graph Generation's panorama spans two extremes: one-shot and sequential models. The former generates nodes and edges jointly, while the latter samples them autoregressively. Each method performs better in different graph domains depending on size and topology, but neither is applicable to all graph categories. For instance, one-shot methods struggle with generating large graphs, while sequential methods underperform on smaller graphs. A possible way to overcome these limitations is to flexibly combine the two methods in a unique system. In this work, we propose the FLAGG (Flexible Autoregressive Graph Generation) framework, which sequentially generates portions of graphs with one-shot models. FLAGG can apply any one-shot model to make it autoregressive, allowing flexibility in choosing the sequential policy. This policy is specified through a stochastic node removal process, which an Insertion Model learns to reverse. We evaluate FLAGG with the DiGress one-shot model on several data sets of different graph sizes and domains. We show that the approach outperforms both one-shot and autoregressive baselines in terms of sampling quality.

</details>


### [185] [RIDE: An Open Dataset and Benchmark for Train Delay Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05070)
*Clément Elliker,Mathis Le Bail,Clément Mantoux,Jesse Read,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RIDE是一个面向比利时全国铁路网络的大规模开源数据集和基准测试平台，包含9450万条火车事件、360万次旅程和3570万条天气记录，标准化了列车延误预测的任务定义、数据划分和评估协议，并通过全面对比实验发现基于学习的方法（尤其是图神经网络）显著优于非学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列车延误预测对乘客和铁路运营商都至关重要，但该领域因缺乏标准化数据集、预测目标和评估协议而难以评估进展。为填补这一空白，作者构建了RIDE基准。

Method: 构建了从原始铁路和天气数据源到两层公共发布（可重用中间关系数据集和模型就绪基准数据集）的分层数据管道；标准化了预测任务、训练/测试数据划分和统一评估协议；对比了非学习方法、统计学习方法和深度学习方法，并按预测时间跨度和延误变化进行细粒度分析。

Result: 基于学习的方法明显优于非学习方法；图神经网络平均性能最佳；最强的学习方法之间性能差距较小；框架不仅提供MAE和RMSE，还按预测时间跨度和延误变化提供细粒度性能分解。

Conclusion: RIDE为列车延误预测提供了标准化的基准和评估框架，实验结果表明基于学习的方法具有显著优势，其中图神经网络表现最优，为后续研究提供了可复现的评估基础。

Abstract: Train delay prediction is an important problem for both passengers and railway operators, yet progress in the field remains difficult to assess due to the lack of standardized datasets, prediction targets, and evaluation protocols. To address this gap, we introduce RIDE, an open dataset and benchmark for train delay prediction built at nationwide scale over the Belgian railway network. RIDE covers 94.5M train events, 3.6M journeys, and 35.7M weather records from 2023 to 2025. It is organized as a layered data pipeline from raw railway and weather sources to two public releases: a reusable intermediate relational dataset and model-ready benchmark datasets. The benchmark standardizes the prediction task and the training and testing data. It also provides a unified evaluation protocol that supports direct comparison across models. Using this framework, we provide the first comprehensive comparative evaluation of non-learning, statistical learning, and deep learning models. We show that learning-based methods clearly outperform non-learning models, with graph neural networks achieving the best mean performance, while the strongest learning-based models remain relatively close to one another. Beyond aggregate mean absolute error (MAE) and root mean squared error (RMSE), the framework also provides breakdowns by prediction horizon and delay change, enabling more detailed analysis of model behavior across forecasting regimes.

</details>


### [186] [Learning What Not to Impute: An Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion Framework for Meaningful Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05073)
*Lixing Zhang,Yidong Ouyang,Weifu Li,Shixiang Zhu,Guang Cheng,Liyan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff-Joint框架，用于区分两种缺失类型（有意义缺失和可插补缺失），通过扩散模型联合建模表格数据与潜在缺失掩码，交替进行条件采样和不确定性感知聚合，实现选择性插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失值插补方法假设所有缺失条目都是可恢复的常规值，但现实数据中缺失可能源于两种不同来源：有些条目是"有意义缺失"（内在不存在且语义有效），而另一些是因观测过程缺失需要插补。现有方法无法区分这两种缺失类型。

Method: 提出Diff-Joint框架，一种基于扩散模型的方法，联合建模表格数据与潜在缺失掩码。该方法交替执行条件采样和不确定性感知聚合，迭代地精炼插补值和缺失标签。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，Diff-Joint有效识别了有意义的缺失条目，同时实现了竞争性的插补准确率和改进的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: Diff-Joint成功解决了选择性插补问题，即在插补常规缺失值的同时保留有意义缺失的原始状态，为处理复杂缺失机制的表格数据提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Missing value imputation is a fundamental task in machine learning, with most existing methods assuming that all missing entries correspond to unobserved regular values. In many real-world datasets, however, missingness may arise from two distinct sources: some entries are meaningfully missing (intrinsically absent and semantically valid), while others are missing due to the observation process and should be imputed. We formalize this distinction as a selective imputation problem, where the goal is to jointly infer which missing entries should be preserved and which should be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose Diff-Joint, a diffusion-based framework that jointly models tabular data together with a latent missingness mask. The method alternates between conditional sampling and uncertainty-aware aggregation to iteratively refine both imputed values and missingness labels. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that Diff-Joint effectively identifies meaningfully missing entries while achieving competitive imputation accuracy and improved downstream task performance.

</details>


### [187] [RePercENT: Scaling Disentangled Representation Learning Beyond Two Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05109)
*Vasiliki Rizou,Pascal Frossard,Dorina Thanou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RePercENT，一种自监督多模态解耦框架，通过即插即用架构和联合优化目标，实现超过两个模态的可扩展成对解耦，无需联合预训练，在保持竞争性能的同时显著降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态解耦方法受限于两模态场景，难以扩展到更多模态，且通常需要联合预训练，计算成本高。

Method: 提出RePercENT框架，采用即插即用架构直接处理预提取的嵌入，无需联合预训练；设计联合优化目标同时学习共享和唯一成分；提供理论最优性保证。

Result: 在多种模态和任务上成功恢复解耦成分，保持竞争性能，显著降低计算复杂度。

Conclusion: RePercENT突破了多模态解耦的可扩展性瓶颈，为超越两模态的通用多模态解耦提供了有效方案。

Abstract: To leverage the full potential of multimodal data, we need representations that go beyond the state-of-the-art alignment and fusion approaches and exploit all cross-modal interactions without sacrificing modality-specific information. Learning disentangled representations is a principled way to identify these underlying shared and unique factors that are hidden in observational data. However, while multimodal disentanglement is a compelling paradigm, existing methods are largely confined to the two-modality regime due to its inherent scalability bottleneck. To address this, we propose RePercENT, a self-supervised framework designed to surpass these limitations and unlocks scalable pairwise disentanglement beyond two modalities. Through a multimodal `plug-and-play' architecture, our approach operates directly on pre-extracted embeddings, eliminating the need for extensive joint pre-training while making no assumptions regarding the underlying modalities or foundation model backbones. Moreover, we introduce a joint optimization objective for simultaneously deriving the shared and unique components, and provide formal theoretical guarantees that characterize the optimality of our solution. Across diverse modalities and tasks, RePercENT successfully recovers disentangled components while maintaining competitive performance and significantly reducing computational complexity.

</details>


### [188] [Graph Set Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05116)
*Jose E. Escrig Molina,Baoquan Chen,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Graph Set Transformer (GST)，一种面向图集合学习的神经网络架构，通过在图每一层中交错进行节点级特征传播与跨图上下文建模，并利用门控机制融合两类信息，在多个基准任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如DeepSets、SetTransformer）需要先用单独的GNN预编码图嵌入，导致特征提取与集合级上下文建模之间存在瓶颈，无法有效融合局部结构与集合上下文信息。

Method: GST在每一层中交错执行节点级特征传播（局部结构建模）和跨图上下文建模（集合级信息融合），并通过门控机制将两个层次的信息融合在一起。

Result: 在合成数据集（集合条件结构推理）和三个真实基准（逐原子反应中心识别、反应产率预测、图像分类）上，在相同参数预算下，GST优于基线方法。消融实验表明，局部与集合上下文的交错融合是性能提升的关键。

Conclusion: GST通过层内交错融合局部图结构与集合上下文，有效解决了现有方法中的信息瓶颈问题，在图集合学习任务上取得了更优性能。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Set Transformer (GST), a neural network architecture for learning on sets of graphs, designed for tasks in which per-element predictions depend on set-wide context as well as local structure. Existing architectures, including DeepSets and SetTransformer, require pre-encoded graph embeddings from a separate GNN, creating a bottleneck between feature extraction and set-level contextualisation. In contrast, GST interleaves node-level feature propagation and cross-graph contextual modelling at every layer, fusing the two levels of information through a gating mechanism. We evaluate GST on a controlled synthetic suite designed to isolate set-conditional structural reasoning and on three real-data benchmarks spanning per-atom reaction-centre identification, reaction yield prediction, and image classification. Under matched parameter budgets, GST performs better than the baselines across these settings. An architectural ablation strongly suggests that the interleaving of local and set context contributes substantially to this advantage.

</details>


### [189] [Generating Financial Time Series by Matching Random Convolutional Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05138)
*Konrad J. Mueller,Nikita Zozoulenko,Ben Wood,Thomas Cass,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SOCK（SOft Competing Kernels），一种全可微分的随机卷积特征映射方法，用于训练生成式时间序列模型。通过匹配真实和生成时间序列的随机卷积特征，SOCK在金融小样本数据集上持续优于基于路径签名和扩散的基线方法，并在双样本假设检验和时间序列分类任务中匹配或超越现有无监督特征映射方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成逼真的金融时间序列极具挑战性，因为训练数据通常仅限于单一历史路径。在如此稀缺的数据下，过拟合难以避免，尤其是在对抗训练中，训练好的判别器可能记忆训练样本。为此，最近的方法通过最小化真实和生成时间序列之间未经训练的特征表示差异来训练生成器，但这些方法基于路径签名的特征映射在可处理的截断深度下可能无法捕获相关的时间序列属性。

Method: 本文提出SOCK（SOft Competing Kernels），一种全可微分的随机卷积特征映射。与现有的非可微分的随机卷积特征映射（如Rocket和Hydra）不同，SOCK能够端到端地用于训练生成模型。通过匹配真实和生成时间序列的随机SOCK特征来训练生成器，避免了对抗训练中判别器过拟合的问题。

Result: 在多种小样本金融数据集上，基于匹配随机SOCK特征训练的生成器持续优于基于路径签名和扩散的基线方法。此外，在双样本假设检验和时间序列分类任务中，SOCK匹配或超越了现有的无监督特征映射方法。

Conclusion: SOCK作为一种全可微分的随机卷积特征映射，为生成式时间序列模型提供了一种有效的监督信号，在金融小样本场景下表现出显著优势，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Generating realistic financial time series is challenging as training data is often limited to a single historical path. With such scarce data, overfitting is hard to avoid, especially under adversarial training where a trained discriminator can memorize the training samples. To mitigate this, recent approaches train generators to minimize the discrepancy between untrained feature representations of real and generated time series. In these works, the feature maps are based on path signatures, which can fail to capture relevant time series properties at tractable truncation depths. In this work, we instead train generators by matching random convolutional features of real and generated time series. Existing random convolutional feature maps, such as Rocket and Hydra, have been shown to provide informative representations of real-world time series, but cannot supervise generative models because they are non-differentiable. We introduce SOCK (SOft Competing Kernels), a fully differentiable random convolutional feature map, suited to train generative time series models. We show that generators trained by matching random SOCK features consistently outperform signature and diffusion baselines across a wide range of small-sample financial datasets. We further demonstrate SOCK's expressiveness on two-sample hypothesis testing and time series classification tasks, where SOCK matches or outperforms existing unsupervised feature maps.

</details>


### [190] [BBOmix: A Tabular Benchmark for Hyperparameter Optimization of Unsupervised Biological Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05139)
*Luca Thale-Bombien,Jan Ewald,Ralf König,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BBOmix，首个面向生物数据无监督表示学习的开源表格基准，包含105,000次评估，涵盖4种自编码器架构和7种多组学数据模态，系统评估了多种超参数优化方法，并揭示了重构损失与下游任务性能之间的弱相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维组学数据日益增多，自编码器虽被广泛用于无监督降维和表示学习，但其对架构和超参数高度敏感，且无监督优化通常依赖重构损失，而这可能无法有效反映下游任务性能。穷举式超参数优化计算成本高昂，研究者常使用次优默认配置。为降低大规模无监督HPO研究门槛，亟需一个标准化的开源基准。

Method: 构建了BBOmix基准，包含来自TCGA和SCHC数据集的7种多组学模态，涵盖4种自编码器架构（共105,000次超参数评估）。量化了重构损失与下游任务性能之间的相关性，并系统评估了单保真度、多保真度和迁移学习三类HPO方法。

Result: 提供了105,000次评估的基准数据，揭示了重构损失与下游任务性能之间的弱相关性，并对多种先进HPO方法进行了全面对比，建立了无监督生物表示学习的基线。

Conclusion: BBOmix为生物信息学领域的无监督表示学习提供了首个标准化开放基准，有助于降低超参数优化的研究门槛，推动该领域的可重复性和方法公平比较。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of high-throughput sequencing has led to large, high-dimensional omics datasets. Deep unsupervised learning architectures, particularly Autoencoders (AEs), are increasingly used for dimensionality reduction and representation learning in this domain. However, AEs are highly sensitive to architectural choices and hyperparameters, and unsupervised optimization typically relies on reconstruction loss, which may be a poor proxy for downstream utility. Exhaustive hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is computationally expensive, leading researchers to frequently rely on suboptimal default configurations. To democratize access to large-scale unsupervised HPO research, we introduce $\textbf{BBOmix}$, the first open-source tabular benchmark for unsupervised representation learning on real-world biological data. Our benchmark includes 105,000 evaluations across four AE architectures and seven multi-omics modalities from the TCGA and SCHC datasets. We quantify the correlation between reconstruction loss and downstream task performance and provide an extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art single-fidelity, multi-fidelity, and transfer learning HPO methods, establishing a rigorous baseline for future research in unsupervised biological representation learning.

</details>


### [191] [STRIDE: Training Data Attribution via Sparse Recovery from Subset Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05165)
*Rishit Dagli,Abir Harrasse,Luke Zhang,Florent Draye,Amirali Abdullah,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STRIDE提出了一种新的训练数据归因方法，通过在激活空间中建模训练数据的功能效应，而非传统的参数空间梯度近似，将问题形式化为压缩感知下的稀疏恢复问题，使用轻量级"转向算子"模拟数据子集训练引起的行为变化，从而高效追溯模型预测与训练数据的关系，在LLM预训练归因任务上达到SOTA且速度提升13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练数据归因方法主要依赖参数空间的梯度近似，但对于LLM而言，跟踪数十亿参数的梯度不仅计算成本极高，而且依赖于局部线性近似，无法准确捕捉训练数据对模型行为的因果影响。需要一种更高效且更精确的归因方法。

Method: STRIDE将训练数据归因问题形式化为激活空间中的稀疏恢复问题。具体而言：(1) 学习轻量级的"转向算子"来模拟在特定数据子集上训练所引起的行为变化；(2) 通过测量这些算子如何扰动测试预测结果；(3) 利用稀疏线性分解恢复单个训练样本的影响力。该方法借鉴了压缩感知的思想。

Result: STRIDE在LLM预训练归因任务上达到了当前最优性能，同时速度比之前的方法快一个数量级（13倍）。在下游应用中（数据选择、数据污染检测、定性分析）也验证了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 在激活空间中建模训练数据的功能效应比传统的参数空间梯度方法更高效且更准确，为LLM训练数据归因提供了新的范式。STRIDE在效率和性能上的双重优势使其具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Training Data Attribution (TDA) seeks to trace a model's predictions back to its training data. The gold standard for TDA relies on causal interventions, observing how a model changes when data is added or removed, but repeated retraining is computationally challenging for Large Language Models (LLMs). Consequently, most approaches approximate this effect in the parameter space using gradients. However, tracking gradients across billions of parameters is not only prohibitively expensive but relies on local approximations. In this work, we propose a shift: rather than estimating parameter changes, we model the functional effect of training data in the activation space. We introduce STRIDE (Steering-based Training Data Influence Decomposition), a framework that formulates TDA as a sparse recovery problem in the spirit of compressive sensing. STRIDE learns lightweight "steering operators" that mimic the behavioral shift caused by training on data subsets. By measuring how these operators perturb test predictions, we recover individual training example influences via sparse linear decomposition. STRIDE achieves state-of-the-art for LLM pre-training attribution while being an order of magnitude ($13\times$) faster than previous art. We further validate its practical utility through downstream applications including data selection, data contamination, and qualitative analysis.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Counterfactual Explanations for Deep Two-Sample Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04009)
*Wei-Cheng Lai,Marco Simnacher,Christoph Lippert*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于反事实解释的深度双样本检验框架，结合扩散自编码器和MMD目标，生成样本级编辑来解释哪些特征驱动了分布差异的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度双样本检验方法虽能提高高维结构化数据上的检测灵敏度，但无法解释哪些数据特征导致了原假设被拒绝，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 结合扩散自编码器与预训练的深度双样本检验模型，在检验模型的表示空间中优化最大均值差异（MMD）目标，生成将源组样本向目标组编辑的反事实样本。

Result: 在合成2D形状数据集和两个MRI队列上，反事实变换一致地提高了p值，表明编辑后的源集在统计上更接近目标分布；通过LPIPS衡量最小性，编辑保持与原始样本接近。

Conclusion: 该方法能提供与已知群体差异一致的可解释编辑，揭示检测到的组间差异所关联的特征，在MRI上局部变化与已知解剖差异一致。

Abstract: Two-sample testing is a fundamental tool for detecting distributional differences across scientific domains, but classical tests (including kernel-based tests) can be ineffective on high-dimensional structured data such as images. Recent deep two-sample tests improve sensitivity in these settings by learning informative representations, yet they provide limited insight into which data features drive rejection of the null hypothesis $H_0$. To address this issue, we propose a counterfactual explanation framework for deep two-sample testing that generates sample-level edits moving observations from a source group toward a target group while explicitly reducing the discrepancy measured by the test. Our method combines a diffusion autoencoder with a pretrained deep two-sample test model and optimizes a maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) objective in the test model's representation space to produce plausible counterfactuals. We quantify distribution-level effects through changes in the test statistic and the resulting two-sample p-values. We evaluate the method on synthetic 2D shape datasets and two MRI cohorts. Across both settings, the counterfactual transformations consistently increase p-values relative to the original samples, indicating that the edited source set becomes statistically closer to the target distribution under the test. We measure minimality using LPIPS to ensure the counterfactuals remain close to the original samples. The resulting edits provide interpretable evidence of the features associated with the detected group differences. On MRI, the localized changes are consistent with known anatomical differences between cohorts.

</details>


### [193] [Finite-Iteration Local Dynamics and Warm Starts for Alternating Power Iteration in Spiked Tensor PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04065)
*Yanjin Xiang,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定阶非对称秩一张量模型的同步交替幂迭代方法，建立了独立于初始化的有限迭代局部收敛理论，并提出了一个通用的单遍原则来分离暖启动机制与特定谱构造，最后通过中心化Gram初始化验证了所需条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为固定阶非对称秩一张量模型中的同步交替幂迭代提供有限步迭代的局部收敛理论，且该理论不依赖于特定的初始化方式，从而弥合全局初始化与局部收敛分析之间的gap。

Method: 1) 建立有限迭代局部理论，将误差分解为几何衰减的瞬态项和由固定正交噪声收缩引起的固有噪声底限；2) 提出通用单遍原则，将暖启动机制与具体谱构造分离；3) 采用信号保持的留一比较法和平均留一片收缩估计（pressed-back估计）验证中心化Gram初始化的相关性和第一遍噪声界。

Result: 在符号兼容的初始化器满足相关性和第一遍噪声水平的条件下，单遍迭代可进入局部邻域，随后局部仿射收缩收敛到该邻域内唯一的有信息局部不动点。对于中心化Gram初始化，在i.i.d.有限四阶矩噪声下验证了所需的相关性和同样本第一遍噪声界。

Conclusion: 本文为张量幂迭代提供了严格的有限样本理论保证，分离了全局初始化与局部收敛的分析，并通过新颖的留一比较技术避免了最坏情况下的非相干性界，适用于更温和的噪声条件。

Abstract: We study simultaneous alternating power iteration for fixed-order asymmetric rank-one spiked tensor models. Our main contribution is a finite-iteration local theory that is independent of any particular initialization. Once the iterates enter a sufficiently small neighborhood of the planted rank-one direction, their error decomposes into a geometrically decaying transient and an intrinsic noise floor caused by fixed orthogonal noise contractions at the planted point. The deterministic finite-sample conditions are stated explicitly, but under a coarse fixed-order multilinear noise event they reduce to a conservative high-signal regime for fixed or slowly expanding local radii.
  We then separate the warm-start mechanism from any specific spectral construction. A generic one-sweep principle shows that, if a sign-compatible initializer has correlation \(γ_N\), first-sweep noise level \(a_N\), and \(a_N/(γ_N^{d-1}ω_{N,d})\to0\), then one can choose an expanding radius \(r_N=o(ω_{N,d})\) for which the first sweep enters the local basin. After entry, the local affine contraction yields convergence to the unique informative local fixed point in that basin. For centered-Gram initialization, we verify the required correlation and same-sample first-sweep noise bound under i.i.d. finite-fourth-moment noise by a signal-preserving noise-only leave-one comparison and an averaged leave-one slice-contraction estimate, which we call a pressed-back estimate. The leave-one comparison keeps the spike fixed and averages over the deleted coordinate, so planted coordinates enter through \(\ell_2\)-weighted sums rather than worst-case incoherence bounds.

</details>


### [194] [REGAIN: REconciliation GAIN-driven Auxiliary Direction Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04380)
*Weijia Li,Shun Hu,Yanfei Kang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出REGAIN框架，通过增广广义最小二乘协调中的辅助线性测量方向，优化最终协调预测的损失，而非传统基于方差或可预测性的选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测协调方法固定测量系统，问"如何将预测投影到一致空间"；本文提出不同问题："哪些额外的线性测量应该被预测并纳入协调系统"，以优化最终协调预测效果。

Method: 提出REGAIN框架：学习归一化辅助方向→用冻结预测器预测诱导序列→通过增广广义最小二乘协调后目标加权损失降低选择方向。配套阶段式学习算法（含留出增益筛选）和可选联合优化步骤。

Result: 在北京PM2.5和澳大利亚旅游数据上，增益选择的测量能改善普通多元预测和层次预测，尤其当它们揭示原始测量系统未捕捉的残差不确定性时效果显著。

Conclusion: 有用的辅助方向必须提供关于未解决目标不确定性的互补信息，而非仅因易于预测而被选择；理论分析阐明了协方差风险降低机制、偏差变化在二次风险中的作用以及估计增益信号的稳定性。

Abstract: Forecast reconciliation usually starts from a fixed measurement system and asks how forecasts should be projected onto a coherent space. We ask a different question: which additional linear measurements should be forecast and included in the reconciliation system? We propose REGAIN, a reconciliation-gain framework that learns normalized auxiliary directions, forecasts the induced series with a frozen forecasting oracle, and selects directions by their target-weighted loss reduction after augmented generalized least-squares reconciliation. Unlike variance-based components or predictability-based auxiliary selection, REGAIN optimizes the downstream effect of an auxiliary measurement on the final reconciled forecasts. We provide a statistical characterization showing that useful auxiliary directions must provide complementary information about unresolved target uncertainty, rather than merely being easy to forecast. The analysis also clarifies the covariance-risk reduction mechanism, the role of bias changes in realized quadratic risk, and the stability of estimated gain signals. A stagewise learning algorithm with held-out gain screening is developed, together with an optional joint refinement step. Experiments on Beijing PM2.5 and Australian Tourism data show that gain-selected measurements can improve both ordinary multivariate and hierarchical forecasts, especially when they reveal residual uncertainty not captured by the original measurement system.

</details>


### [195] [Knockoffs-based False Discovery Rate Control and Simplification for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04404)
*Huiqi Zhang,Wenyu Liao,Yiqing Shi,Xiaobo Huang,Fang Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文基于knockoff方法和正则化神经网络，提出了三种在控制错误发现率条件下的变量筛选方法（单层过滤器、多层过滤器、变量权重聚合过滤器），用于解决深度神经网络中无关参数和输入变量导致的计算复杂度和成本增加问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络常包含大量与目标无关的参数和输入变量，这不仅增加了计算复杂度，也带来了额外的计算成本，亟需有效的变量筛选方法来控制错误发现率。

Method: 基于knockoff方法，结合正则化神经网络，提出了三种变量筛选方法：单层过滤器（one layer filter）、多层过滤器（multiple layers filter）、变量权重聚合过滤器（variable weight aggregation filter），均在控制错误发现率的条件下进行。

Result: 与现有算法相比，所提三种算法均表现出令人满意的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的三种变量筛选方法能有效控制错误发现率，同时降低深度神经网络中无关变量带来的计算开销，性能优于现有算法。

Abstract: The deep neural network is a widely used framework in machine learning that has been widely applied in various fields. However, deep neural networks often involve a large number of parameters and inputs, many of which may be irrelevant to the goal or true output. These parameters and \textcolor{black}{input variables} not only increase computational complexity, but also contribute to additional computational cost. One solution to this problem is knockoff methods, which have proven successful in controlling false discovery rates in high-dimensional regression. Building on the knockoff methods and using the regularised neural network, this paper proposes three variable screening methods under the condition of controlling false discovery rates: \textit{one layer filter}, \textit{multiple layers filter}, \textit{variable weight aggregation filter}. In comparison with existing algorithms, we find that our algorithms show satisfactory performance.

</details>


### [196] [Flatness and Generalization: Learning Multi-Index Models with Homogeneous Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04429)
*Harsh Vardhan,Hossein Taheri,Arya Mazumdar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究非凸神经网络中平坦性与泛化之间的关系，证明在两层齐次神经网络学习多指标模型时，"最平坦插值器"（在所有插值器中具有最小平坦度）能够泛化，而某些非泛化插值器的平坦度无法通过对称性变得接近最平坦值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有"平坦插值器泛化好"的启发式观点被Dinh等人(2017)质疑，因为网络对称性可以在不改变损失的情况下任意改变平坦度。本文旨在重新建立平坦性与泛化之间的严格联系，解释该启发式观点为何在实践中仍然有效。

Method: 论文通过理论分析研究两层齐次神经网络学习未知多指标模型。首先构造一类非泛化插值器，证明其平坦度无法通过对称性接近最平坦值；然后证明在低近似误差和低标签噪声条件下，最平坦插值器总能达到小的群体损失。

Result: (1) 存在一类非泛化插值器，其平坦度无法通过对称性变得接近最平坦可能值；(2) 对于单指标模型和生成的数据，在近似误差和标签噪声较低时，最平坦插值器总能实现小群体损失，即最平坦插值器总是泛化的。

Conclusion: 平坦性与泛化之间存在直接联系，该联系适用于一大类激活函数和真实数据分布，关键在于关注"最平坦插值器"而非任意插值器的平坦度。

Abstract: A common heuristic used to explain the generalization of first-order gradient methods on non-convex neural networks is that "flat interpolators generalize well" (Hochreiter and Schmidhuber, 1994; Keskar et al., 2017), where flatness can be measured by the trace of the Hessian of the empirical loss. However, Dinh et al. 2017) showed that, using symmetry of the network that can change flatness while keeping the population and empirical losses unchanged, any interpolator can be made sharper or flatter. This result makes the earlier heuristic statement vacuous. In this paper, we show that for learning an unknown multi-index model with $2$-layer non-convex homogeneous neural networks, there is a connection between flatness and generalization, despite the existence of symmetries. This connection pertains to the "flattest" interpolators, i.e., the interpolators that have orderwise minimum flatness among all interpolators. First, we show that there exists a natural class of non-generalizing interpolators whose flatness cannot be made closer to the flattest possible, even using symmetries. Second, we show that for data generated by a sum of single-index models, if the approximation error and label noise are low, any flattest interpolator achieves small population loss, i.e., the flattest interpolators always generalize. This establishes a direct link between flatness and generalization which applies to a large class of activations and realistic data distributions.

</details>


### [197] [ReSGA: A Large Tail Risk Model for Learning Value-at-Risk and Expected Shortfall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04576)
*Yichi Zhang,Ke Zhu,Zhoufan Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出检索增强自分组自编码器（ReSGA），一个拥有数百万参数的大规模尾部风险模型，利用153个公司特征对1926-2023年美国股票收益进行VaR和ES预测。ReSGA在样本外损失和统计回测上优于12个竞争对手，其预测优势可转化为显著的经济收益。系统规模分析表明，联合VaR-ES预测改进主要源于数据复杂性而非模型复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有尾部风险模型（VaR和ES）参数有限，在大数据时代容易遭受模型误设，无法充分利用资产间的横截面依赖性和长期时间动态。

Method: 提出检索增强自分组自编码器（ReSGA），一种拥有数百万参数的大规模神经网络模型，利用153个公司特征对1926-2023年的美国股票月度收益进行建模，捕捉资产的横截面依赖性和时间动态。

Result: ReSGA在样本外损失和统计回测中优于12个计量经济学和机器学习竞争对手；预测优势可转化为长短期十等分组合的显著经济收益；系统规模分析显示改进主要来自数据复杂性而非模型复杂性；群体重要性和迁移学习分析展示了可解释性和跨市场泛化能力。

Conclusion: 大规模参数模型能有效改进尾部风险预测，数据复杂性是驱动预测提升的关键因素，ReSGA具有良好的可解释性和跨市场泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning Value-at-Risk (VaR) and Expected Shortfall (ES) is important for managing financial risks effectively. Existing approaches with limited parameters are vulnerable to model misspecification in the era of big data. To address this limitation, we propose a large tail risk model, the retrieval-enhanced self-grouping autoencoder (ReSGA), which is designed with millions of parameters to exploit the rich cross-sectional dependence and long-term temporal dynamics of assets using their characteristics. Applied to monthly US equity returns from 1926 to 2023 with 153 firm characteristics, ReSGA outperforms twelve econometric and machine learning competitors in terms of out-of-sample loss and statistical backtesting. In addition, its forecast advantages can translate into significant economic gains from long-short decile portfolios that are constructed by a new size-enhanced left-side momentum strategy. To clarify the role of complexity, we further conduct a systematic scaling analysis and demonstrate that improvements in joint VaR-ES forecasting are primarily driven by data complexity rather than model complexity. Finally, our analyses of group-importance and transfer-learning exhibit the interpretability and cross-market generalizability of ReSGA.

</details>


### [198] [Bayesian learning for the stochastic shortest path problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04845)
*Chon Wai Ho,Sumeetpal S. Singh,Jiaqi Guo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对随机最短路径问题，提出了一种贝叶斯框架来学习最优动作价值函数Q*，通过Bellman最优性方程直接构建后验分布，解决了现有方法依赖不现实假设和近似的问题，并展示了更好的数据效率和不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯方法在学习马尔可夫决策过程的最优策略时，往往依赖于不现实的建模假设和特设的近似方法，缺乏一种严格且通用的贝叶斯框架来学习最优动作价值函数Q*。

Method: 通过Bellman最优性方程直接构建Q*的后验信念；对于确定性奖励，表征后验为具有流形密度的分布；为简化推理，放宽似然使其存在Lebesgue密度（但引入不可识别性问题）；针对表格化Q*参数化、高斯似然松弛和高斯先验，计算了最优动作选择的精确后验概率。

Result: 在Deep Sea基准的变体上进行的数值研究验证了理论发现；该框架能忠实地量化不确定性；与其他基于时序差分学习的贝叶斯方法相比，具有更高的数据效率。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架为SSP问题提供了一种更严谨的不确定性量化方法，未来工作可在此基础上进一步拓展。

Abstract: Sequential decision-making problems are often modelled as a Markov decision process (MDP). We focus on the stochastic shortest path (SSP) problem, which is an infinite-horizon undiscounted MDP with absorbing terminal states. We develop a Bayesian framework to learn the optimal decision strategy through interactions with the decision-making task. Specifically, we learn the optimal action-value function $Q^*$, but unlike many existing Bayesian approaches, we do not rely on unrealistic modelling assumptions and ad-hoc approximations. Our approach is to directly construct the posterior beliefs for $Q^*$ through Bellman's optimality equations. For deterministic rewards, we characterise the posterior as a distribution with a manifold density. To facilitate simpler inference, we relax the likelihood so that a Lebesgue density exists. The flip side is to create unidentifiability issues. Specifically, the relaxed posterior can have significant mass on improper decision rules, while the exact posterior will not.
  We also calculate the exact posterior probabilities for optimal action selections for the tabular parametrisation of $Q^*$, a Gaussian likelihood relaxation and a Gaussian prior, which is useful in benchmarking studies. Numerical studies on variants of the Deep Sea benchmark verify our findings. We demonstrate that our framework faithfully quantifies uncertainty and, compared to other temporal-difference-based Bayesian methodologies, is more data efficient. We conclude with recommendations for future work.

</details>
